## `site/examples/buttons/index.html`

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
   <meta charset="UTF-8">
   <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
   <title>ShaderMate – shader buttons</title>
   <link rel="stylesheet" href="../../assets/site.css">
   <style>
      main { min-height: calc(100vh - 56px); display: grid; place-content: center; gap: 28px; padding: 40px 0 }
      .head { text-align: center }
      .head h1 { margin: 0; color: var(--text-strong); font-size: 28px; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: -.03em }
      .head p { margin: 8px 0 0; color: var(--text-dim); font-size: 14px }
      .row { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 240px); gap: 16px }
      @media (max-width: 820px) { .row { grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr); width: calc(100vw - 48px) } }

      /* A real <button>; the canvas is just its background. */
      .shader-btn {
         position: relative; overflow: hidden; isolation: isolate;
         height: 64px; border: 1px solid var(--border-strong); border-radius: 12px;
         font: 600 15px var(--ui-font); color: #fff; cursor: pointer;
         background: var(--surface); box-shadow: 0 10px 30px -14px #000;
         transition: transform .15s, box-shadow .15s, border-color .15s;
      }
      .shader-btn:hover { transform: translateY(-2px); box-shadow: 0 16px 36px -16px #000; border-color: #4a4a4a }
      .shader-btn:active { transform: translateY(0) scale(.985) }
      .shader-btn canvas { position: absolute; inset: 0; width: 100%; height: 100%; z-index: -1 }
      .shader-btn span { text-shadow: 0 1px 2px #000a }
      .note { text-align: center; color: var(--label); font-size: 12.5px }
      .note code { color: var(--text-dim) }
   </style>
</head>
<body>
   <main>
      <div class="head"><h1>Shader buttons</h1><p>Hover, press and click. Each button owns a tiny canvas + shader; the label is normal DOM.</p></div>
      <div class="row">
         <button class="shader-btn" data-fx="plasma"><canvas></canvas><span>Plasma</span></button>
         <button class="shader-btn" data-fx="ripple"><canvas></canvas><span>Ripple</span></button>
         <button class="shader-btn" data-fx="liquid"><canvas></canvas><span>Liquid</span></button>
         <button class="shader-btn" data-fx="holo"><canvas></canvas><span>Holographic</span></button>
         <button class="shader-btn" data-fx="aurora"><canvas></canvas><span>Aurora</span></button>
         <button class="shader-btn" data-fx="neon"><canvas></canvas><span>Neon edge</span></button>
      </div>
      <p class="note">Pointer tracking is scoped to each button (<code>pointer: { target: el }</code>), so a shader only reacts while you're over it.</p>
   </main>

   <script type="module">
      import { createShader, glsl } from '../../lib/shadermate.esm.js';
      import { mountNav } from '../../assets/nav.js';
      mountNav();

      // Shared uniforms + helpers, pulled into every button shader via `includes`.
      // Built-ins (u_time, u_mouseUV, u_aspect …) are declared automatically; these three are ours.
      const common = glsl`
         uniform float u_hover;   // 0..1, tweened on pointer enter / leave
         uniform float u_press;   // 0..1, tweened on pointer down / up
         uniform vec3  u_click;   // click position (UV) and the time it happened
         float hash(vec2 p) { return fract(sin(dot(p, vec2(127.1, 311.7))) * 43758.5453); }
         float noise(vec2 p) {
            vec2 i = floor(p), f = fract(p); f = f * f * (3.0 - 2.0 * f);
            return mix(mix(hash(i), hash(i + vec2(1, 0)), f.x), mix(hash(i + vec2(0, 1)), hash(i + 1.0), f.x), f.y);
         }
         float fbm(vec2 p) { float v = 0.0, a = 0.5; for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) { v += a * noise(p); p = p * 2.0 + 3.1; a *= 0.5; } return v; }
         vec3 pal(float t) { return 0.5 + 0.5 * cos(6.2831 * (t + vec3(0.0, 0.33, 0.67))); }
         // mouse in button UV, but only while hovered (eases back to the centre otherwise)
         vec2 mouseUV() { return mix(vec2(0.5), u_mouseUV, u_hover); }
         // signed distance to a rounded-rect border, in pixels, for edge effects
         float edgeDist(vec2 fc, float radius) {
            vec2 h = u_resolution * 0.5; vec2 q = abs(fc - h) - (h - radius);
            return length(max(q, 0.0)) + min(max(q.x, q.y), 0.0) - radius;   // 0 at the edge, negative inside
         }
      `;

      const effects = {
         // Sine plasma that brightens and speeds up on hover.
         plasma: glsl`
            #include "common.glsl"
            void mainImage(out vec4 o, in vec2 fc) {
               vec2 uv = fc / u_resolution; vec2 p = uv * vec2(u_aspect, 1.0) * 3.0;
               float t = u_time * (0.6 + u_hover * 1.2);
               vec2 m = mouseUV() * vec2(u_aspect, 1.0) * 3.0;
               float v = sin(p.x + t) + sin(p.y + t * 0.7) + sin((p.x + p.y) * 0.7 + t * 1.3) + sin(length(p - m) * 3.0 - t);
               vec3 col = pal(v * 0.15 + 0.1) * (0.35 + 0.35 * u_hover + 0.3 * u_press);
               o = vec4(col, 1.0);
            }`,

         // Dark glass with rings expanding from each click (u_click = x, y in UV, time).
         ripple: glsl`
            #include "common.glsl"
            void mainImage(out vec4 o, in vec2 fc) {
               vec2 uv = fc / u_resolution; vec2 asp = vec2(u_aspect, 1.0);
               vec3 base = mix(vec3(0.10, 0.12, 0.30), vec3(0.35, 0.15, 0.55), uv.x + 0.2 * noise(uv * 3.0 + u_time * 0.3));
               float age = u_time - u_click.z;
               float d = distance(uv * asp, u_click.xy * asp);
               float ring = smoothstep(0.03, 0.0, abs(d - age * 1.1)) * exp(-age * 2.5);
               float ring2 = smoothstep(0.02, 0.0, abs(d - age * 0.7)) * exp(-age * 3.0) * 0.6;
               float glow = smoothstep(0.6, 0.0, distance(uv * asp, mouseUV() * asp)) * u_hover * 0.35;
               vec3 col = base * (0.7 + 0.3 * u_hover + 0.2 * u_press) + vec3(0.6, 0.8, 1.0) * (ring + ring2) + vec3(0.5, 0.6, 1.0) * glow;
               o = vec4(col, 1.0);
            }`,

         // Metaballs drifting around, pulled toward the cursor while hovered.
         liquid: glsl`
            #include "common.glsl"
            float ball(vec2 p, vec2 c, float r) { return r / dot(p - c, p - c); }
            void mainImage(out vec4 o, in vec2 fc) {
               vec2 uv = fc / u_resolution; vec2 p = uv * vec2(u_aspect, 1.0);
               vec2 m = mouseUV() * vec2(u_aspect, 1.0);
               float t = u_time;
               vec2 c1 = vec2(0.8 + 0.5 * sin(t * 0.9), 0.5 + 0.3 * cos(t * 1.3));
               vec2 c2 = vec2(2.2 + 0.6 * cos(t * 0.7), 0.5 + 0.35 * sin(t * 1.1));
               vec2 c3 = vec2(1.5 + 0.9 * sin(t * 0.5), 0.5 + 0.3 * sin(t * 1.7));
               c1 = mix(c1, m, u_hover * 0.5); c2 = mix(c2, m, u_hover * 0.3);
               float f = ball(p, c1, 0.09) + ball(p, c2, 0.08) + ball(p, c3, 0.06) + ball(p, m, 0.05 * u_hover);
               float edge = smoothstep(0.9, 1.1, f);
               float rim = smoothstep(0.9, 1.0, f) - smoothstep(1.0, 1.8, f);
               vec3 col = mix(vec3(0.06, 0.10, 0.16), mix(vec3(0.0, 0.7, 0.6), vec3(0.2, 0.4, 1.0), uv.x), edge) + rim * vec3(0.9, 1.0, 1.0) * 0.5;
               col += 0.15 * u_press;
               o = vec4(col, 1.0);
            }`,

         // Holographic foil: hue depends on view angle (cursor) + fine grain.
         holo: glsl`
            #include "common.glsl"
            void mainImage(out vec4 o, in vec2 fc) {
               vec2 uv = fc / u_resolution; vec2 m = mouseUV();
               float angle = (uv.x - m.x) * 1.5 + (uv.y - m.y) * 0.6;
               float grain = noise(fc * 0.6) * 0.08;
               float bands = sin((uv.x * 1.2 + uv.y * 0.4 + angle + u_time * 0.1) * 6.2831) * 0.5 + 0.5;
               vec3 col = pal(angle * 0.6 + bands * 0.15 + grain);
               float lit = 0.45 + 0.45 * u_hover + 0.2 * u_press;
               col = mix(vec3(0.9), col, 0.6) * lit + grain * 0.15;
               o = vec4(col, 1.0);
            }`,

         // Aurora curtains: fbm bands drifting sideways, teal / green / violet.
         aurora: glsl`
            #include "common.glsl"
            void mainImage(out vec4 o, in vec2 fc) {
               vec2 uv = fc / u_resolution; float t = u_time * 0.25;
               float x = uv.x * u_aspect * 1.2 + (mouseUV().x - 0.5) * 0.6 * u_hover;
               float a = fbm(vec2(x * 1.5 + t, uv.y * 0.6 + t * 0.3));
               float b = fbm(vec2(x * 2.5 - t * 0.7, uv.y * 0.4 + 7.0));
               float curtain = smoothstep(0.2, 0.85, a + b * 0.5 - uv.y * 0.35);
               vec3 col = vec3(0.02, 0.05, 0.10);
               col += curtain * mix(vec3(0.05, 0.9, 0.55), vec3(0.45, 0.25, 0.95), b) * (0.7 + 0.5 * u_hover + 0.25 * u_press);
               col += pow(curtain, 4.0) * vec3(0.6, 1.0, 0.9) * 0.25;
               o = vec4(col, 1.0);
            }`,

         // Neon edge: a glowing gradient running around the border, chasing the cursor on hover.
         neon: glsl`
            #include "common.glsl"
            void mainImage(out vec4 o, in vec2 fc) {
               vec2 uv = fc / u_resolution;
               float d = edgeDist(fc, 11.0);
               vec2 c = uv - 0.5; vec2 m = mouseUV() - 0.5;
               float ang = atan(c.y, c.x * u_aspect) / 6.2831;                   // -0.5..0.5 around the centre
               float mang = atan(m.y, m.x * u_aspect) / 6.2831;
               float sweep = fract(ang - u_time * 0.15);                           // rotating highlight
               float chase = exp(-pow(fract(ang - mang + 0.5) - 0.5, 2.0) * 60.0) * u_hover; // highlight near the cursor
               vec3 hue = pal(ang + u_time * 0.1);
               float line = exp(-abs(d + 1.5) * 0.9);                              // thin bright edge
               float glow = exp(-abs(d + 1.5) * 0.12);                             // soft spill inward
               float energy = 0.35 + 0.5 * pow(sweep, 3.0) + chase + 0.3 * u_press;
               vec3 col = vec3(0.07, 0.08, 0.11);
               col += hue * (line * 1.2 + glow * 0.25) * energy;
               o = vec4(col, 1.0);
            }`,
      };

      /** Wire one <button>: shader background + hover / press / click uniforms. */
      function shaderButton(btn, frag) {
         const sm = createShader({
            canvas: btn.querySelector('canvas'),
            shader: frag,
            includes: { 'common.glsl': common },
            pointer: { target: btn, smooth: 0.2 },        // only this button's pointer events
            uniforms: { u_hover: 0, u_press: 0, u_click: [0.5, 0.5, -10] },
         });
         btn.addEventListener('pointerenter', () => sm.tween('u_hover', 1, { duration: 350, ease: 'outCubic' }));
         btn.addEventListener('pointerleave', () => sm.tween('u_hover', 0, { duration: 700, ease: 'outExpo' }));
         btn.addEventListener('pointerdown', () => sm.tween('u_press', 1, { duration: 80 }));
         btn.addEventListener('pointerup', () => sm.tween('u_press', 0, { duration: 500, ease: 'outElastic' }));
         btn.addEventListener('pointerleave', () => sm.tween('u_press', 0, { duration: 300 }));
         btn.addEventListener('click', e => {
            const [u, v] = sm.domToUV(e.clientX, e.clientY);      // click position in the canvas' UV space
            sm.set('u_click', [u, v, sm.time]);
         });
         return sm;
      }

      for (const btn of document.querySelectorAll('.shader-btn')) shaderButton(btn, effects[btn.dataset.fx]);
   </script>
</body>
</html>
```

## `site/examples/buttons/thumb.glsl`

```glsl
void mainImage(out vec4 o,in vec2 fc){vec2 uv=fc/u_resolution;vec2 p=uv*vec2(u_aspect,1.)*3.;float t=u_time*.7;
            float v=sin(p.x+t)+sin(p.y+t*.7)+sin((p.x+p.y)*.7+t*1.3);vec3 c=(.5+.5*cos(6.2831*(v*.15+vec3(0.,.33,.67))))*.45;o=vec4(c,1.);}
```

## `site/examples/canvas-texture/index.html`

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
   <meta charset="UTF-8">
   <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
   <title>ShaderMate – canvas as texture</title>
   <link rel="stylesheet" href="../../assets/site.css">
   <style>
      html, body { height: 100%; overflow: hidden; background: #000 }
      #out { width: 100%; height: 100%; display: block }
   </style>
</head>
<body>
   <canvas id="out"></canvas>
   <a class="back" href="../"><span class="brand-mark"></span>Examples</a>
   <div class="hint">a 2D canvas clock, redrawn every frame, distorted by a shader · move the mouse</div>

   <script type="module">
      import { createShader, glsl } from '../../lib/shadermate.esm.js';

      // 1. An ordinary 2D canvas we draw into with the Canvas API. It never touches the DOM.
      const text = document.createElement('canvas');
      text.width = 1024; text.height = 512;
      const ctx = text.getContext('2d');
      function drawClock() {
         const now = new Date();
         const hh = String(now.getHours()).padStart(2, '0'), mm = String(now.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0'), ss = String(now.getSeconds()).padStart(2, '0');
         ctx.clearRect(0, 0, text.width, text.height);
         ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
         ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
         ctx.font = '600 230px Geist, system-ui, -apple-system, "Segoe UI", sans-serif';
         ctx.fillText(`${hh}:${mm}`, 512, 230);
         ctx.font = '500 70px "Geist Mono", ui-monospace, Menlo, Consolas, monospace';
         ctx.fillStyle = '#9ff';
         ctx.fillText(`:${ss}.${String(now.getMilliseconds()).padStart(3, '0').slice(0, 2)}`, 512, 420);
      }

      // 2. Feed it to a shader as iChannel0. Canvases are re-uploaded every frame automatically.
      const sm = createShader({
         canvas: '#out',
         pointer: { smooth: 0.12 },
         passes: [{
            shader: glsl`
               float hash(vec2 p) { return fract(sin(dot(p, vec2(127.1, 311.7))) * 43758.5453); }
               void mainImage(out vec4 o, in vec2 fc) {
                  vec2 uv = fc / iResolution.xy;
                  vec2 m = iMouse.xy / iResolution.xy;
                  float t = iTime;

                  // fit the 2:1 text canvas into the viewport, centred
                  float texAspect = iChannelResolution[0].x / iChannelResolution[0].y;
                  vec2 p = (uv - 0.5) * vec2(u_aspect / texAspect, 1.0) * 1.15 + 0.5;

                  // ripples spreading from the cursor + a slow wave
                  float d = distance(uv * vec2(u_aspect, 1.0), m * vec2(u_aspect, 1.0));
                  float ripple = sin(d * 40.0 - t * 5.0) * exp(-d * 3.0) * 0.012;
                  p += ripple * normalize(uv - m + 1e-4);
                  p.x += sin(p.y * 12.0 + t * 1.5) * 0.004;

                  // RGB split along the ripple direction
                  vec2 shift = vec2(0.006 + 0.01 * exp(-d * 3.0), 0.0);
                  float r = texture(iChannel0, p + shift).r;
                  float g = texture(iChannel0, p).g;
                  float b = texture(iChannel0, p - shift).b;
                  vec3 txt = vec3(r, g, b);

                  // background: soft gradient + scanlines + grain
                  vec3 bg = mix(vec3(0.03, 0.04, 0.10), vec3(0.10, 0.05, 0.20), uv.y);
                  bg += 0.06 * vec3(0.3, 0.9, 1.0) * exp(-d * 2.5);
                  float scan = 0.9 + 0.1 * sin(fc.y * 1.5 + t * 8.0);
                  vec3 col = bg + txt * vec3(1.0, 0.95, 0.9) * scan;
                  col += (hash(fc + t) - 0.5) * 0.04;
                  o = vec4(col, 1.0);
               }`,
            channels: [text],                 // <- the 2D canvas
         }],
      });

      // 3. Redraw the 2D canvas before every frame.
      sm.on('beforeFrame', drawClock);
   </script>
</body>
</html>
```

## `site/examples/fragcoord/sketch.js`

```js
// The whole thing below is one string in the FragCoord.xyz export format:
// `// Common`, `// Pass 1`, `// Main` headers, `void main()` + fragColor, u_* uniforms,
// vec4 u_mouse, float u_scroll, u_pass1 / u_main buffers and `//` uniform annotations.
// ShaderMate splits it into passes and wires everything up.

const sketch = `// Common
#define TAU 6.28318530718
vec2 rot(vec2 p, float a){ float c = cos(a), s = sin(a); return mat2(c, -s, s, c) * p; }

// Pass 1 — reaction / feedback
uniform float u_fade = 0.985;   //[0.9, 1.0]
uniform float u_swirl = 0.6;    //[0.0, 2.0]
uniform vec3  u_ink;            //0.25, 0.75, 1.0, [0.0, 1.0]

void main() {
   vec2 res = u_resolution;
   vec2 uv = gl_FragCoord.xy / res;
   vec2 p = (gl_FragCoord.xy - 0.5 * res) / res.y;
   float zoom = exp(-u_scroll * 0.2);                      // FragCoord's wheel accumulator

   // advect the previous frame (u_pass1 = this pass, last frame)
   vec2 q = p * zoom;
   float a = u_swirl * 0.03 * sin(u_time * 0.5 + length(q) * 6.0);
   vec2 back = rot(q, a) / zoom;
   vec2 flow = vec2(sin(q.y * 7.0 + u_time), cos(q.x * 7.0 - u_time)) * 0.0015;
   vec4 prev = texture(u_pass1, (back + flow) * vec2(res.y / res.x, 1.0) + 0.5);

   // ink follows the mouse while the button is down (u_mouse.z >= 0)
   vec2 m = (u_mouse.xy - 0.5 * res) / res.y;
   float held = step(0.0, u_mouse.z);
   float d = length(p - m);
   float ink = held * smoothstep(0.08, 0.0, d);
   vec3 col = prev.rgb * u_fade + ink * u_ink;

   // a quiet emitter in the middle so there's something to look at before you draw
   float em = smoothstep(0.03, 0.0, length(p - 0.25 * vec2(cos(u_time * 0.7), sin(u_time * 0.9))));
   col += em * 0.6 * (0.5 + 0.5 * cos(u_time + vec3(0.0, 2.0, 4.0)));
   fragColor = vec4(col, 1.0);
}

// Main
uniform float u_glow = 1.2;     //[0.0, 3.0]

void main() {
   vec2 uv = gl_FragCoord.xy / u_resolution;
   vec3 c = texture(u_pass1, uv).rgb;
   vec3 g = vec3(0.0);
   for (int i = -2; i <= 2; i++) for (int j = -2; j <= 2; j++)
      g += texture(u_pass1, uv + vec2(i, j) * 2.0 / u_resolution).rgb;
   c += g / 25.0 * u_glow * 0.5;
   c = c / (1.0 + c);                                        // tone map
   c = pow(c, vec3(0.8));
   fragColor = vec4(c, 1.0);
}`;

const sm = ShaderMate.createShader({
   passes: sketch,                    // one pasted blob → Common + Pass 1 + Main
   size: 'window',
   statsKey: 'F1',
});
ShaderMate.createGui(sm, { title: 'FragCoord paste' });   // sliders come from the //[min, max] annotations
```

## `site/examples/hero/index.html`

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
   <meta charset="UTF-8">
   <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
   <title>ShaderMate – hero banner</title>
   <link rel="stylesheet" href="../../assets/site.css">
   <style>
      .hero { position: relative; height: 86vh; min-height: 460px; overflow: hidden; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border) }
      .hero canvas { width: 100%; height: 100%; display: block }   /* ShaderMate follows this box */
      .hero .copy { position: absolute; inset: 0; display: grid; place-content: center; text-align: center; pointer-events: none; padding: 0 20px }
      .hero .tag { justify-self: center; height: 22px; padding: 0 9px; display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border-radius: 11px; background: rgba(120, 190, 255, .16); color: var(--w-sky); font-size: 12px; font-weight: 500 }
      .hero h1 { margin: 14px 0 0; color: #fff; font-size: clamp(44px, 7.5vw, 92px); font-weight: 600; line-height: .98; letter-spacing: -.04em }
      .hero p { margin: 18px auto 0; max-width: 46ch; color: #d7e6f7; font-size: clamp(15px, 1.8vw, 18px) }
      .cta { pointer-events: auto; display: inline-flex; gap: 10px; margin-top: 28px; justify-content: center }
      main { padding: 64px 0 120px }
      main h2 { margin: 0 0 10px; color: var(--text-strong); font-size: 26px; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: -.03em }
      main p { max-width: 70ch; color: var(--text-dim) }
   </style>
</head>
<body>
   <section class="hero">
      <canvas id="hero"></canvas>
      <div class="copy">
         <span class="tag">Hero banner</span>
         <h1>Shaders,<br>anywhere.</h1>
         <p>A luminous wave field in one fragment shader: it follows the cursor, swells when you hover the button, and parallaxes as you scroll.</p>
         <div class="cta">
            <button class="btn btn-primary" id="shot">Save a screenshot</button>
            <a class="btn btn-quiet" href="https://github.com/Tezumie/ShaderMate">View source</a>
         </div>
      </div>
   </section>

   <main class="wrap">
      <h2>How this works</h2>
      <p>The canvas is an ordinary element styled with CSS (<code>width: 100%; height: 86vh</code>); ShaderMate sizes its bitmap to match and keeps it in sync on resize. The headline and buttons are normal DOM on top of it. The pointer still reaches the shader because ShaderMate listens on the window and maps coordinates into the canvas.</p>
      <p>Hovering the primary button tweens <code>u_hover</code>, which raises the waves' amplitude and glow. <code>u_scroll</code> shifts the field as you scroll. Rendering pauses when the hero leaves the viewport or the tab is hidden, so the page stays cheap.</p>
      <p style="height: 50vh"></p>
   </main>

   <script type="module">
      import { createShader, glsl } from '../../lib/shadermate.esm.js';
      import { mountNav } from '../../assets/nav.js';
      mountNav({ overlay: true });

      const sm = createShader({
         canvas: '#hero',
         pointer: { smooth: 0.1 },
         uniforms: { u_hover: 0 },
         shader: glsl`
            uniform float u_hover;

            float hash(vec2 p) { return fract(sin(dot(p, vec2(127.1, 311.7))) * 43758.5453); }
            float noise(vec2 p) {
               vec2 i = floor(p), f = fract(p); f = f * f * (3.0 - 2.0 * f);
               return mix(mix(hash(i), hash(i + vec2(1, 0)), f.x), mix(hash(i + vec2(0, 1)), hash(i + 1.0), f.x), f.y);
            }
            float fbm(vec2 p) { float v = 0.0, a = 0.5; for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) { v += a * noise(p); p = p * 2.1 + 3.7; a *= 0.5; } return v; }

            void mainImage(out vec4 o, in vec2 fc) {
               vec2 uv = fc / u_resolution;
               float x = uv.x * u_aspect;
               vec2 m = u_mouseUV * vec2(u_aspect, 1.0);
               float t = u_time * 0.35;
               float scroll = u_scroll.y / u_resolution.y;

               // background: #181818 at the top fading into Codevre navy
               vec3 col = mix(vec3(0.094), vec3(0.043, 0.153, 0.271), smoothstep(1.0, 0.1, uv.y));

               // wave palette: indigo, deep blue, blue, teal (back to front)
               vec3 fill[4]; fill[0] = vec3(0.216, 0.188, 0.639); fill[1] = vec3(0.039, 0.298, 0.549); fill[2] = vec3(0.0, 0.471, 0.831); fill[3] = vec3(0.090, 0.635, 0.722);
               vec3 line = vec3(0.471, 0.745, 1.0);                       // #78beff

               // cursor influence: a ripple that lifts the waves near the mouse
               float near = exp(-pow(x - m.x, 2.0) * 1.5);
               float amp = 1.0 + 0.8 * u_hover;

               for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
                  float fi = float(i);
                  float base = 0.50 - 0.11 * fi - scroll * (0.15 + 0.05 * fi);
                  float y = base
                     + (0.045 + 0.012 * fi) * amp * sin(x * (1.6 + 0.35 * fi) + t * (0.9 + 0.2 * fi) + fi * 1.9)
                     + 0.035 * amp * (fbm(vec2(x * 1.8 + fi * 3.0, t * 0.6)) - 0.5)
                     + 0.06 * near * sin(t * 3.0 + fi) * (0.4 + 0.6 * u_hover);
                  float d = uv.y - y;
                  // fill below the curve, darker with depth
                  col = mix(col, fill[i] * (0.45 + 0.1 * fi), smoothstep(0.003, -0.003, d) * 0.92);
                  // bright line + soft bloom
                  col += line * exp(-abs(d) * 180.0) * (0.55 + 0.45 * u_hover);
                  col += fill[i] * exp(-abs(d) * 22.0) * (0.22 + 0.2 * u_hover);
               }

               // soft light at the cursor + grain
               col += vec3(0.3, 0.6, 1.0) * 0.08 * exp(-distance(vec2(x, uv.y), m) * 2.5) * (0.5 + u_hover);
               col += (hash(fc + u_time) - 0.5) * 0.03;
               o = vec4(col, 1.0);
            }`,
      });

      const shot = document.getElementById('shot');
      shot.addEventListener('pointerenter', () => sm.tween('u_hover', 1, { duration: 500, ease: 'outCubic' }));
      shot.addEventListener('pointerleave', () => sm.tween('u_hover', 0, { duration: 1100, ease: 'outExpo' }));
      shot.addEventListener('click', () => sm.download('hero.png'));
   </script>
</body>
</html>
```

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>

   <link rel="stylesheet" href="../assets/site.css">
   <style>
      header.hero { position: relative; height: 46vh; min-height: 300px; overflow: hidden; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border) }
      header.hero canvas { position: absolute; inset: 0; width: 100%; height: 100% }
      header.hero .wrap { position: relative; height: 100%; display: flex; flex-direction: column; justify-content: flex-end; padding-bottom: 40px; pointer-events: none }
      .tag { align-self: flex-start; height: 22px; padding: 0 9px; display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border-radius: 11px; background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent) 18%, transparent); color: var(--link); font-size: 12px; font-weight: 500 }

      header.hero p { margin: 10px 0 0; max-width: 56ch; color: #d7e6f7; font-size: 16px }
      main { padding: 36px 0 0 }
   </style>
</head>
<body>
   <!--NAV current=Examples overlay-->
   <header class="hero">
      <canvas id="bg"></canvas>
      <div class="wrap">
         <span class="tag">Examples</span>
         <h1>Drop a shader on anything.</h1>
         <p>Every example is a plain HTML page. Open one, view source, copy what you need.</p>
      </div>
   </header>

   <main class="wrap">
      <div class="section-head"><h2>Examples</h2><span><!--COUNT--> examples · <a href="../docs/contributing/" style="color:var(--link)">add yours</a></span></div>
      <div class="cards">
<!--GALLERY-->
      </div>
   </main>

   <!--FOOTER-->

   <script type="module">
      import { createShader, glsl } from '../lib/shadermate.esm.js';

      // Header: Codevre's wave palette, slow and quiet behind the text.
      createShader({
         canvas: '#bg', scale: 0.6, maxFps: 30,
         shader: glsl`
            float h(vec2 p){ return fract(sin(dot(p, vec2(127.1, 311.7))) * 43758.5453); }
            float n(vec2 p){ vec2 i = floor(p), f = fract(p); f = f*f*(3.-2.*f);
               return mix(mix(h(i), h(i+vec2(1,0)), f.x), mix(h(i+vec2(0,1)), h(i+1.), f.x), f.y); }
            void mainImage(out vec4 o, in vec2 fc) {
               vec2 uv = fc / u_resolution; float t = u_time * .18;
               vec3 col = mix(vec3(.094), vec3(.043, .153, .271), smoothstep(.0, 1., uv.y));     // #181818 -> #0b2745
               vec3 cols[4]; cols[0] = vec3(.216,.188,.639); cols[1] = vec3(.039,.298,.549); cols[2] = vec3(0.,.471,.831); cols[3] = vec3(.09,.635,.722);
               for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
                  float fi = float(i);
                  float y = .08 + .1 * fi + .05 * sin(uv.x * (2.2 + .5 * fi) * u_aspect + t * (.8 + .2 * fi) + fi * 1.7) + .04 * (n(vec2(uv.x * 3. * u_aspect + fi, t)) - .5);
                  float d = uv.y - y;
                  col = mix(col, cols[i] * .55, smoothstep(.004, -.004, d) * .9);
                  col += vec3(.47, .745, 1.) * exp(-abs(d) * 160.) * .5 + cols[i] * exp(-abs(d) * 25.) * .25;
               }
               o = vec4(col, 1.);
            }`,
      });

   </script>
   <!--THUMBS-->
   <script type="module" src="../assets/gallery.js"></script>
</body>
</html>
```

## `site/examples/livecode/index.html`

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
   <meta charset="UTF-8">
   <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
   <title>ShaderMate – live coding</title>
   <link rel="stylesheet" href="../../assets/site.css">
   <style>
      body { height: 100vh; display: grid; grid-template-rows: 48px 1fr; grid-template-columns: minmax(320px, 46%) 1fr; overflow: hidden }
      .toolbar { grid-column: 1 / -1 }
      textarea { width: 100%; height: 100%; padding: 16px; border: 0; border-right: 1px solid var(--border); resize: none; background: var(--surface); color: var(--text-strong); font: 13px/1.55 var(--mono-font); outline: none; tab-size: 4 }
      canvas { width: 100%; height: 100%; display: block; background: #000 }
      @media (max-width: 700px) { body { grid-template-columns: 1fr; grid-template-rows: 48px 1fr 1fr } textarea { border-right: 0; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border) } }
   </style>
</head>
<body>
   <header class="toolbar">
      <a class="title" href="../"><span class="brand-mark" style="width:16px;height:16px;border-radius:4px"></span>Live GLSL</a>
      <select class="select" id="preset"></select>
      <button class="btn btn-quiet btn-sm" id="shot">Screenshot</button>
      <button class="btn btn-quiet btn-sm" id="rec">Record 4s</button>
      <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" id="pause">Pause</button>
      <span class="meta" id="fps"></span>
   </header>
   <textarea id="code" spellcheck="false"></textarea>
   <canvas id="out"></canvas>

   <script type="module">
      import { createShader } from '../../lib/shadermate.esm.js';

      const presets = {
         'Tunnel': `void mainImage(out vec4 o, in vec2 fc) {
    vec2 p = (fc - 0.5 * iResolution.xy) / iResolution.y;
    float a = atan(p.y, p.x), r = length(p);
    vec2 uv = vec2(0.3 / r + iTime * 0.6, a / 3.1416 + iTime * 0.1);
    float f = sin(uv.x * 12.0) * sin(uv.y * 12.0);
    vec3 col = 0.5 + 0.5 * cos(iTime + uv.xyx * 4.0 + vec3(0, 2, 4));
    col *= smoothstep(-0.2, 0.6, f) * r * 2.5;
    o = vec4(col, 1.0);
}`,
         'Raymarched sphere': `float sdf(vec3 p) {
    p.xz *= mat2(cos(iTime), -sin(iTime), sin(iTime), cos(iTime));
    float box = length(max(abs(p) - 0.7, 0.0)) - 0.1;
    float sph = length(p) - 1.0;
    return mix(box, sph, 0.5 + 0.5 * sin(iTime * 0.8));
}
vec3 normal(vec3 p) { vec2 e = vec2(0.002, 0); return normalize(vec3(sdf(p+e.xyy)-sdf(p-e.xyy), sdf(p+e.yxy)-sdf(p-e.yxy), sdf(p+e.yyx)-sdf(p-e.yyx))); }
void mainImage(out vec4 o, in vec2 fc) {
    vec2 p = (fc - 0.5 * iResolution.xy) / iResolution.y;
    vec3 ro = vec3(0, 0, -3.5), rd = normalize(vec3(p, 1.6));
    float t = 0.0; vec3 col = vec3(0.02, 0.02, 0.05) + p.y * 0.05;
    for (int i = 0; i < 80; i++) {
        vec3 q = ro + rd * t; float d = sdf(q);
        if (d < 0.001) { vec3 n = normal(q); vec3 l = normalize(vec3(1, 2, -2));
            float dif = max(dot(n, l), 0.0); float spec = pow(max(dot(reflect(-l, n), -rd), 0.0), 32.0);
            col = vec3(0.9, 0.4, 0.3) * dif + spec + 0.1 * n.y + 0.05; break; }
        if (t > 10.0) break; t += d;
    }
    o = vec4(col, 1.0);
}`,
         'Plasma': `void mainImage(out vec4 o, in vec2 fc) {
    vec2 uv = fc / iResolution.xy * 4.0;
    float t = iTime;
    float v = sin(uv.x + t) + sin(uv.y + t * 0.7) + sin((uv.x + uv.y) * 0.5 + t) + sin(length(uv - 2.0) * 2.0 - t);
    vec3 col = 0.5 + 0.5 * cos(v + vec3(0, 2, 4) + iMouse.x / iResolution.x * 3.0);
    o = vec4(col, 1.0);
}`,
         'FragCoord.xyz style': `// Written the way FragCoord.xyz shaders are: own main(), fragColor, vec4 u_mouse.
uniform vec2 u_resolution;
uniform vec4 u_mouse;      // xy: position, zw: click position (negative while the button is up)
uniform float u_scroll;    // mouse-wheel accumulator: exp(u_scroll) = zoom

void main() {
    vec2 p = (gl_FragCoord.xy - 0.5 * u_resolution) / u_resolution.y * exp(-u_scroll);
    vec2 m = (u_mouse.xy - 0.5 * u_resolution) / u_resolution.y;
    float t = u_time;
    float d = length(p - m * 0.5);
    float rings = sin(d * 30.0 - t * 4.0) * 0.5 + 0.5;
    float press = step(0.0, u_mouse.z);
    vec3 col = (0.5 + 0.5 * cos(t + d * 6.0 + vec3(0, 2, 4))) * rings;
    col = mix(col, vec3(1.0) - col, press * 0.5);
    fragColor = vec4(col, 1.0);
}`,
         'Voronoi': `vec2 h2(vec2 p) { return fract(sin(vec2(dot(p, vec2(127.1, 311.7)), dot(p, vec2(269.5, 183.3)))) * 43758.5453); }
void mainImage(out vec4 o, in vec2 fc) {
    vec2 p = fc / iResolution.y * 6.0;
    vec2 i = floor(p), f = fract(p);
    float d1 = 9.0, d2 = 9.0;
    for (int y = -1; y <= 1; y++) for (int x = -1; x <= 1; x++) {
        vec2 g = vec2(x, y); vec2 c = g + 0.5 + 0.45 * sin(iTime + 6.28 * h2(i + g));
        float d = length(c - f); if (d < d1) { d2 = d1; d1 = d; } else if (d < d2) d2 = d;
    }
    float edge = smoothstep(0.0, 0.06, d2 - d1);
    vec3 col = mix(vec3(1.0, 0.9, 0.6), vec3(0.1, 0.2, 0.5) + 0.4 * d1, edge);
    o = vec4(col, 1.0);
}`,
      };

      const code = document.getElementById('code');
      const sel = document.getElementById('preset');
      for (const name in presets) sel.append(new Option(name, name));
      code.value = presets[sel.value];

      // errorOverlay: true draws compile errors over the canvas (default 'auto' = only on localhost)
      const sm = createShader({ canvas: '#out', shader: code.value, errorOverlay: true });

      let timer;
      const reload = () => sm.setShader(code.value).catch(() => { /* shown in the overlay */ });
      code.addEventListener('input', () => { clearTimeout(timer); timer = setTimeout(reload, 250); });
      code.addEventListener('keydown', e => {           // Tab inserts spaces instead of leaving the box
         if (e.key === 'Tab') { e.preventDefault(); const s = code.selectionStart; code.setRangeText('    ', s, code.selectionEnd, 'end'); }
      });
      sel.addEventListener('change', () => { code.value = presets[sel.value]; reload(); });

      document.getElementById('shot').onclick = () => sm.download('livecode.png');
      document.getElementById('rec').onclick = async e => {
         e.target.disabled = true; e.target.textContent = 'Recording…';
         try { await sm.record({ duration: 4000, download: 'livecode.webm' }); }
         finally { e.target.disabled = false; e.target.textContent = 'Record 4s'; }
      };
      const pause = document.getElementById('pause');
      pause.onclick = () => { sm.toggle(); pause.textContent = sm.playing ? 'Pause' : 'Play'; };

      // fps readout, smoothed
      let acc = 0, n = 0;
      sm.on('afterFrame', ({ fps }) => { acc += fps; n++; });
      setInterval(() => { if (n) document.getElementById('fps').textContent = (acc / n).toFixed(0) + ' fps'; acc = n = 0; }, 500);
   </script>
</body>
</html>
```

## `site/examples/multipass/flow.glsl`

```glsl
// Advect the previous frame through a curl-noise field and inject ink.
uniform float u_decay;
uniform float u_speed;
uniform float u_scaleN;
uniform float u_brush;
uniform bool  u_emit;
uniform float u_clear;

float hash(vec2 p) { return fract(sin(dot(p, vec2(127.1, 311.7))) * 43758.5453); }
float noise(vec2 p) {
    vec2 i = floor(p), f = fract(p); f = f * f * (3.0 - 2.0 * f);
    return mix(mix(hash(i), hash(i + vec2(1, 0)), f.x), mix(hash(i + vec2(0, 1)), hash(i + 1.0), f.x), f.y);
}
float fbm(vec2 p) { float v = 0.0, a = 0.5; for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) { v += a * noise(p); p = p * 2.0 + 7.3; a *= 0.5; } return v; }

// curl of a scalar noise field = divergence-free velocity (swirly, no sinks)
vec2 curl(vec2 p) {
    float e = 0.02;
    float dx = fbm(p + vec2(e, 0.0)) - fbm(p - vec2(e, 0.0));
    float dy = fbm(p + vec2(0.0, e)) - fbm(p - vec2(0.0, e));
    return vec2(dy, -dx) / (2.0 * e);
}
vec3 pal(float t) { return 0.5 + 0.5 * cos(6.2831 * (t + vec3(0.0, 0.33, 0.67))); }

void mainImage(out vec4 o, in vec2 fc) {
    vec2 uv = fc / iResolution.xy;
    vec2 asp = vec2(iResolution.x / iResolution.y, 1.0);
    float t = iTime * 0.15;

    // where did the ink at this pixel come from?
    vec2 vel = curl(uv * asp * u_scaleN + t) * u_speed;
    vec2 src = uv - vel * 0.0025;
    vec4 prev = texture(iChannel0, src) * u_decay;

    // paint with the mouse
    vec2 m = iMouse.xy / iResolution.xy;
    if (iMouse.z > 0.0) {
        float d = distance(uv * asp, m * asp);
        prev.rgb += pal(iTime * 0.1 + m.x) * smoothstep(u_brush, 0.0, d) * 0.9;
    }

    // two orbiting emitters so the piece is alive without interaction
    if (u_emit) {
        vec2 e1 = 0.5 + 0.32 * vec2(cos(iTime * 0.6), sin(iTime * 0.9));
        vec2 e2 = 0.5 + 0.28 * vec2(sin(iTime * 0.5 + 2.0), cos(iTime * 0.7 + 1.0));
        prev.rgb += pal(iTime * 0.07) * smoothstep(0.05, 0.0, distance(uv * asp, e1 * asp)) * 0.25;
        prev.rgb += pal(iTime * 0.07 + 0.5) * smoothstep(0.05, 0.0, distance(uv * asp, e2 * asp)) * 0.25;
    }

    if (u_clear > 0.5 || iFrame < 2) prev = vec4(0.0);
    o = vec4(prev.rgb, 1.0);
}
```

## `site/examples/multipass/image.glsl`

```glsl
// Tone-map the flow buffer and add a cheap glow.
uniform float u_exposure;
uniform float u_glow;

void mainImage(out vec4 o, in vec2 fc) {
    vec2 uv = fc / iResolution.xy;
    vec3 c = texture(iChannel0, uv).rgb;

    // 5-tap blur for glow (the buffer is half-res + linear, so taps are cheap and soft)
    vec2 px = 4.0 / iResolution.xy;
    vec3 g = texture(iChannel0, uv + vec2(px.x, 0.0)).rgb + texture(iChannel0, uv - vec2(px.x, 0.0)).rgb
           + texture(iChannel0, uv + vec2(0.0, px.y)).rgb + texture(iChannel0, uv - vec2(0.0, px.y)).rgb;
    c += g * 0.25 * u_glow;

    c *= u_exposure;
    c = c / (1.0 + c);                      // Reinhard
    c = pow(c, vec3(0.9));
    c *= 1.0 - 0.4 * dot(uv - 0.5, uv - 0.5); // vignette
    o = vec4(c, 1.0);
}
```

## `site/examples/multipass/sketch.js`

```js
// Flow field: ink is advected through a curl-noise velocity field inside a ping-pong
// float buffer (pass "flow"), then a second pass tone-maps it to the screen.
//
//   flow  -> half-resolution RGBA16F, pingpong, reads its own previous frame
//   image -> full-resolution screen pass, reads "flow"

const sm = ShaderMate.createShader({
   passes: [
      {
         name: 'flow',
         shader: 'flow.glsl',
         size: 0.5,                        // simulate at half res: cheap and plenty smooth
         pingpong: true,
         float: true,
         filter: 'linear',
         channels: ['flow'],               // previous frame
         uniforms: {
            u_decay: { value: 0.992, min: 0.9, max: 1.0, step: 0.001, label: 'Decay' },
            u_speed: { value: 1.0, min: 0, max: 3, label: 'Flow speed' },
            u_scaleN: { value: 2.5, min: 0.5, max: 8, label: 'Noise scale' },
            u_brush: { value: 0.05, min: 0.01, max: 0.2, label: 'Brush size' },
            u_emit: { value: true, label: 'Auto emitters' },
         },
      },
      {
         name: 'image',
         shader: 'image.glsl',
         channels: ['flow'],
         uniforms: {
            u_exposure: { value: 1.4, min: 0.2, max: 4, label: 'Exposure' },
            u_glow: { value: 0.6, min: 0, max: 2, label: 'Glow' },
         },
      },
   ],
   stats: 'key',                           // F1 toggles the stats overlay
   statsPosition: 'bottom-right',
});

ShaderMate.createGui(sm, { title: 'Flow field' });

addEventListener('keydown', e => {
   if (e.key === 'c') { sm.set('u_clear', 1); setTimeout(() => sm.set('u_clear', 0), 50); }
});
```

```glsl
float h(vec2 p){return fract(sin(dot(p,vec2(127.1,311.7)))*43758.5453);} float n(vec2 p){vec2 i=floor(p),f=fract(p);f=f*f*(3.-2.*f);return mix(mix(h(i),h(i+vec2(1,0)),f.x),mix(h(i+vec2(0,1)),h(i+1.),f.x),f.y);}
            void mainImage(out vec4 o,in vec2 fc){vec2 uv=fc/u_resolution*vec2(u_aspect,1.);float t=u_time*.2;float a=n(uv*3.+t)*6.2831;vec2 d=vec2(cos(a),sin(a));
            float s=sin(dot(uv,d)*18.+t*4.);vec3 c=(.5+.5*cos(6.2831*(n(uv*2.-t)+vec3(0.,.33,.67))))*smoothstep(.2,1.,s)*.7;o=vec4(c,1.);}
```

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>

  vec2 uv = (fragCoord - 0.5 * iResolution.xy) / iResolution.y;
  float d = length(uv - 0.3 * vec2(cos(iTime), sin(iTime * 1.3)));
  vec3 col = 0.5 + 0.5 * cos(iTime + uv.xyx * 3.0 + vec3(0, 2, 4));

}</textarea></div>

            <div class="feature"><h3>Paste and run</h3><p>ShaderToy's <code>mainImage</code>, <code>iMouse</code>, Common and Buffers, and FragCoord.xyz's <code>void main</code>, <code>u_*</code> uniforms and whole multi-pass exports.</p></div>
            <div class="feature"><h3>Real multi-pass</h3><p>Ping-pong float buffers, feedback, per-pass size, and video, webcam, audio, keyboard or any canvas as input.</p></div>
            <div class="feature"><h3>Built for pages</h3><p>Follows your CSS size, pauses off-screen, caps FPS, and tracks the pointer per element. Heroes and buttons are first-class.</p></div>

/&gt;</code></pre></div>

<!--FEATURED-->
         </div>

         <div class="banner">
            <div><h2>Coming from ShaderToy or FragCoord.xyz?</h2><p>Copy the shader, or the whole multi-pass export, and pass it to <code>createShader</code>. Uniform comments like <code>//[0, 5]</code> become GUI sliders.</p></div>
            <a class="btn btn-primary btn-lg" href="docs/shadertoy-fragcoord/">How compatibility works</a>
         </div>

   <!--FOOTER-->

   <script type="module">
      import { createShader, glsl } from './lib/shadermate.esm.js';

      // Hero: Codevre's wave palette.
      createShader({
         canvas: '#bg', scale: 0.7, maxFps: 45,
         shader: glsl`
            float h(vec2 p){ return fract(sin(dot(p, vec2(127.1, 311.7))) * 43758.5453); }
            float n(vec2 p){ vec2 i = floor(p), f = fract(p); f = f*f*(3.-2.*f);
               return mix(mix(h(i), h(i+vec2(1,0)), f.x), mix(h(i+vec2(0,1)), h(i+1.), f.x), f.y); }
            void mainImage(out vec4 o, in vec2 fc) {
               vec2 uv = fc / u_resolution; float t = u_time * .18;
               vec3 col = mix(vec3(.094), vec3(.043, .153, .271), smoothstep(.0, 1., uv.y));
               vec3 cols[4]; cols[0] = vec3(.216,.188,.639); cols[1] = vec3(.039,.298,.549); cols[2] = vec3(0.,.471,.831); cols[3] = vec3(.09,.635,.722);
               for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
                  float fi = float(i);
                  float y = .16 + .12 * fi + .06 * sin(uv.x * (2.2 + .5 * fi) * u_aspect + t * (.8 + .2 * fi) + fi * 1.7) + .05 * (n(vec2(uv.x * 3. * u_aspect + fi, t)) - .5);
                  float d = uv.y - y;
                  col = mix(col, cols[i] * .55, smoothstep(.004, -.004, d) * .9);
                  col += vec3(.47, .745, 1.) * exp(-abs(d) * 160.) * .5 + cols[i] * exp(-abs(d) * 25.) * .25;
               }
               col *= smoothstep(1.05, .35, length((uv - vec2(.5, .45)) * vec2(1.2, 1.))) * .5 + .5;
               o = vec4(col, 1.);
            }`,
      });

      // Live editor: the textarea is the shader. Compile errors show inline.
      const code = document.getElementById('code'), err = document.getElementById('err');
      const demo = createShader({ canvas: '#demo', shader: code.value, maxFps: 60, errorOverlay: false, pointer: false });
      demo.on('error', e => { err.hidden = false; err.textContent = String(e.message || e).split('\n').slice(0, 6).join('\n'); });
      demo.on('shader', () => { err.hidden = true; });
      let timer;
      code.addEventListener('input', () => { clearTimeout(timer); timer = setTimeout(() => demo.setShader(code.value), 250); });
      code.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
         if (e.key === 'Tab') { e.preventDefault(); const s = code.selectionStart; code.setRangeText('  ', s, code.selectionEnd, 'end'); code.dispatchEvent(new Event('input')); }
      });

      document.getElementById('copy-install').addEventListener('click', async e => {
         try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText('npm i shadermate'); e.target.textContent = 'Copied'; } catch { e.target.textContent = 'Select & copy'; }
         setTimeout(() => { e.target.textContent = 'Copy'; }, 1400);
      });
   </script>
   <script type="module" src="assets/gallery.js"></script>
</body>
</html>
```

## `src/capture/capture.js`

```js
/**
 * Screenshots and video capture. `renderNow()` draws a fresh frame synchronously, so these
 * work without preserveDrawingBuffer (the pixels are read in the same task as the draw).
 */
export function createCapture(canvas, renderNow) {
   function screenshot(type = 'image/png', quality) {
      renderNow();
      return canvas.toDataURL(type, quality);
   }

   function toBlob(type = 'image/png', quality) {
      renderNow();
      return new Promise((res, rej) => canvas.toBlob(b => (b ? res(b) : rej(new Error('toBlob failed'))), type, quality));
   }

   async function download(filename = 'shadermate.png', type, quality) {
      if (!type) type = /\.jpe?g$/i.test(filename) ? 'image/jpeg' : /\.webp$/i.test(filename) ? 'image/webp' : 'image/png';
      const blob = await toBlob(type, quality);
      saveBlob(blob, filename);
   }

   /**
    * Record the canvas to a WebM (or whatever the browser supports).
    *   const rec = sm.record({ fps: 60 });  …  const blob = await rec.stop();
    *   await sm.record({ duration: 5000, download: 'loop.webm' });
    */
   function record({ fps = 60, mimeType, bitsPerSecond = 8_000_000, duration = 0, download: dl } = {}) {
      if (typeof MediaRecorder === 'undefined' || !canvas.captureStream) throw new Error('MediaRecorder not supported');
      const stream = canvas.captureStream(fps);
      const type = mimeType || ['video/webm;codecs=vp9', 'video/webm;codecs=vp8', 'video/webm', 'video/mp4']
         .find(t => MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported(t)) || '';
      const rec = new MediaRecorder(stream, { mimeType: type || undefined, videoBitsPerSecond: bitsPerSecond });
      const chunks = [];
      rec.ondataavailable = e => { if (e.data.size) chunks.push(e.data); };
      const done = new Promise(res => { rec.onstop = () => res(new Blob(chunks, { type: rec.mimeType })); });
      rec.start(100);

      const handle = {
         recorder: rec,
         stop() { if (rec.state !== 'inactive') rec.stop(); stream.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop()); return done; },
         done
      };
      if (duration > 0) {
         const finished = new Promise(res => setTimeout(() => res(handle.stop()), duration));
         if (dl) return finished.then(blob => { saveBlob(blob, typeof dl === 'string' ? dl : 'shadermate.webm'); return blob; });
         return finished;
      }
      return handle;
   }

   return { screenshot, toBlob, download, record };
}

export function saveBlob(blob, filename) {
   const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
   const a = document.createElement('a');
   a.href = url; a.download = filename;
   document.body.appendChild(a);
   a.click();
   a.remove();
   setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
}
```
