## `src/gui/index.js`

```js
// Optional uniform inspector built on lil-gui (MIT). Separate entry so the core stays dependency-free.
//
//   import { createGui } from 'shadermate/gui';      // npm: `npm i lil-gui`
//   <script src="dist/shadermate-gui.js"></script>   // browser: bundles lil-gui, adds ShaderMate.createGui()
//
//   const gui = await createGui(sm, { title: 'Hero', controls: true });
//
// Uniform hints (on a uniform spec):  { value, min, max, step, label, color: true, hidden: true }

import GUI from 'lil-gui';
import { resolveTheme } from '../ui/theme.js';

const isArrayLike = v => Array.isArray(v) || (ArrayBuffer.isView(v) && !(v instanceof DataView));
const isSpec = v => v != null && typeof v === 'object' && !isArrayLike(v) && 'value' in v;

function describe(entry) {
   const spec = isSpec(entry) ? entry : { value: entry };
   if (spec.hidden) return null;
   const v = spec.value;
   const t = typeof spec.type === 'string' ? spec.type.toLowerCase() : '';
   if (/mat|matrix|sampler/.test(t)) return null;
   if (typeof v === 'boolean' || t === 'bool' || t === 'boolean') return { kind: 'bool', spec };
   if (typeof v === 'number') return { kind: /int|^\d?i/.test(t) ? 'int' : 'float', spec };
   if (isArrayLike(v) && v.length >= 2 && v.length <= 4 && typeof v[0] === 'number') {
      return { kind: spec.color && v.length >= 3 ? 'color' : 'vec', n: v.length, spec };
   }
   return null;
}

function range(value, spec, isInt) {
   const abs = Math.abs(value) || 1;
   const max = spec.max ?? (isInt ? Math.max(10, abs * 2) : abs * 2);
   const min = spec.min ?? (value < 0 ? -max : 0);
   const step = spec.step ?? (isInt ? 1 : (max - min) / 200);
   return { min, max, step };
}

function addUniform(folder, sm, name, entry, passName) {
   const d = describe(entry);
   if (!d) return;
   const { spec } = d;
   const label = spec.label || name;
   const proxy = {};
   const push = v => sm.set(name, v, passName);

   if (d.kind === 'bool') {
      proxy[name] = !!spec.value;
      folder.add(proxy, name).name(label).onChange(push);
   } else if (d.kind === 'float' || d.kind === 'int') {
      proxy[name] = spec.value;
      const r = range(spec.value, spec, d.kind === 'int');
      folder.add(proxy, name, r.min, r.max, r.step).name(label).onChange(push).listen();
      // keep slider in sync with tweens / sm.set()
      Object.defineProperty(proxy, name, { get: () => sm.get(name, passName), set: push });
   } else if (d.kind === 'color') {
      proxy[name] = Array.from(spec.value).slice(0, 3);
      const alpha = spec.value.length === 4 ? spec.value[3] : null;
      folder.addColor(proxy, name).name(label).onChange(c => push(alpha === null ? Array.from(c) : [...c, alpha]));
   } else {
      const sub = folder.addFolder(label);
      const comps = ['x', 'y', 'z', 'w'].slice(0, d.n);
      const cur = Array.from(spec.value);
      comps.forEach((c, i) => {
         proxy[c] = cur[i];
         const r = range(cur[i], spec, false);
         sub.add(proxy, c, r.min, r.max, r.step).onChange(v => { cur[i] = v; push(cur.slice()); });
      });
   }
}

/**
 * Build the panel. Resolves to the lil-gui instance once the ShaderMate instance is ready.
 * opts: { title, container, open, controls (play/pause, time scale, capture), uniforms (true | string[]) }
 */
export async function createGui(sm, opts = {}) {
   await sm.ready;
   const gui = new GUI({ title: opts.title || 'ShaderMate', container: opts.container, width: opts.width || 280 });
   if (opts.open === false) gui.close();
   if (opts.theme !== false) applyTheme(gui, resolveTheme(opts.theme === undefined ? sm.theme : opts.theme));
   sm.on('destroy', () => gui.destroy());

   if (opts.controls !== false) {
      const c = gui.addFolder('Playback');
      const ctl = {
         get playing() { return sm.playing; }, set playing(v) { v ? sm.play() : sm.pause(); },
         get timeScale() { return sm.timeScale; }, set timeScale(v) { sm.timeScale = v; },
         get time() { return sm.time; }, set time(v) { sm.seek(v); },
         screenshot: () => sm.download(),
         record5s: () => sm.record({ duration: 5000, download: 'shadermate.webm' }),
         stats: () => sm.stats.toggle(),
      };
      c.add(ctl, 'playing').listen();
      c.add(ctl, 'timeScale', -2, 4, 0.01).listen();
      c.add(ctl, 'time').listen().decimals(2);
      c.add(ctl, 'screenshot');
      c.add(ctl, 'record5s').name('record 5s');
      c.add(ctl, 'stats');
      c.close();
   }

   const only = Array.isArray(opts.uniforms) ? new Set(opts.uniforms) : null;
   if (opts.uniforms !== false) {
      const seen = new Set();
      const addAll = (folder, map, passName) => {
         for (const name in map) {
            if (only && !only.has(name)) continue;
            if (seen.has(name)) continue;
            seen.add(name);
            addUniform(folder, sm, name, map[name], passName);
         }
      };
      addAll(gui, sm.uniforms);
      for (const po of sm.passes) {
         if (!Object.keys(po.cfg.uniforms).length) continue;
         addAll(sm.passes.length > 1 ? gui.addFolder(po.name) : gui, po.cfg.uniforms, sm.passes.length > 1 ? po.name : undefined);
      }
   }
   return gui;
}

/** Skin lil-gui through its CSS variables so it matches ShaderMate's overlays (dark editor palette). */
export function applyTheme(gui, T) {
   const el = gui.domElement;
   const vars = {
      '--background-color': T.bg, '--text-color': T.text, '--title-background-color': T.bgAlt, '--title-text-color': T.textStrong,
      '--widget-color': T.raised, '--hover-color': T.hover, '--focus-color': T.border, '--number-color': T.number, '--string-color': T.string,
      '--font-family': T.font, '--font-size': '12px', '--input-font-size': '12px', '--font-family-mono': T.mono,
      '--padding': '6px', '--spacing': '4px', '--widget-height': '22px', '--title-height': '28px', '--name-width': '42%',
      '--widget-border-radius': '5px', '--checkbox-size': '14px', '--slider-knob-width': '3px', '--folder-indent': '10px',
   };
   for (const k in vars) el.style.setProperty(k, vars[k]);
   Object.assign(el.style, { border: `1px solid ${T.border}`, borderRadius: T.radius, overflow: 'hidden', boxShadow: T.shadow });
   if (!gui.parent && !el.style.position) { el.style.top = '12px'; el.style.right = '12px'; }
   return gui;
}

export { GUI };
```

export { glsl, ShaderMateError } from './utils/util.js';
export { EASES } from './core/tween.js';

## `src/input/camera.js`

```js
/**
 * Fly camera for `u_camera_pos` / `u_camera_dir` / `u_camera_view` (FragCoord.xyz convention).
 *   WASD / arrows move, Q/E (or Space/Shift) down/up, mouse drag looks around, wheel dollies.
 *   u_camera_view is the world-to-camera matrix, column-major (OpenGL convention), so
 *   mat3(u_camera_view) * dir goes world->camera and transpose goes back.
 */
export function createCamera(canvas, { target = window, speed = 1.5, sensitivity = 0.004 } = {}) {
   const pos = [0, 0, 0];
   let yaw = -Math.PI / 2, pitch = 0;       // looking down -Z
   const keys = new Set();
   let dragging = false, lastX = 0, lastY = 0;
   const dir = [0, 0, -1];
   const view = new Float32Array(16);

   const onKey = (e, down) => {
      const k = e.key.length === 1 ? e.key.toLowerCase() : e.key;
      if (down) keys.add(k); else keys.delete(k);
   };
   const kd = e => onKey(e, true), ku = e => onKey(e, false);
   const pd = e => { if (e.button === 0) { dragging = true; lastX = e.clientX; lastY = e.clientY; } };
   const pm = e => {
      if (!dragging) return;
      yaw += (e.clientX - lastX) * sensitivity;
      pitch -= (e.clientY - lastY) * sensitivity;
      pitch = Math.max(-1.55, Math.min(1.55, pitch));
      lastX = e.clientX; lastY = e.clientY;
   };
   const pu = () => { dragging = false; };
   const wh = e => { const f = dir; const d = -e.deltaY * 0.002 * speed; pos[0] += f[0] * d; pos[1] += f[1] * d; pos[2] += f[2] * d; };
   const blur = () => keys.clear();

   target.addEventListener('keydown', kd); target.addEventListener('keyup', ku);
   canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', pd); window.addEventListener('pointermove', pm); window.addEventListener('pointerup', pu);
   canvas.addEventListener('wheel', wh, { passive: true }); window.addEventListener('blur', blur);

   function lookAt() {
      const f = dir;
      // right = normalize(cross(f, up)), up' = cross(right, f)
      const upW = [0, 1, 0];
      let r = [f[1] * upW[2] - f[2] * upW[1], f[2] * upW[0] - f[0] * upW[2], f[0] * upW[1] - f[1] * upW[0]];
      const rl = Math.hypot(r[0], r[1], r[2]) || 1; r = r.map(v => v / rl);
      const u = [r[1] * f[2] - r[2] * f[1], r[2] * f[0] - r[0] * f[2], r[0] * f[1] - r[1] * f[0]];
      // column-major world->camera
      view.set([
         r[0], u[0], -f[0], 0,
         r[1], u[1], -f[1], 0,
         r[2], u[2], -f[2], 0,
         -(r[0] * pos[0] + r[1] * pos[1] + r[2] * pos[2]),
         -(u[0] * pos[0] + u[1] * pos[1] + u[2] * pos[2]),
         (f[0] * pos[0] + f[1] * pos[1] + f[2] * pos[2]),
         1,
      ]);
      return { right: r, up: u };
   }

   return {
      pos, dir, view,
      /** Advance by dt seconds. */
      update(dt) {
         dir[0] = Math.cos(pitch) * Math.cos(yaw); dir[1] = Math.sin(pitch); dir[2] = Math.cos(pitch) * Math.sin(yaw);
         const { right } = lookAt();
         const v = speed * dt * (keys.has('Shift') ? 3 : 1);
         const mv = (vec, s) => { pos[0] += vec[0] * s; pos[1] += vec[1] * s; pos[2] += vec[2] * s; };
         if (keys.has('w') || keys.has('ArrowUp')) mv(dir, v);
         if (keys.has('s') || keys.has('ArrowDown')) mv(dir, -v);
         if (keys.has('d') || keys.has('ArrowRight')) mv(right, v);
         if (keys.has('a') || keys.has('ArrowLeft')) mv(right, -v);
         if (keys.has('e') || keys.has(' ')) pos[1] += v;
         if (keys.has('q') || keys.has('c')) pos[1] -= v;
         lookAt();
      },
      dispose() {
         target.removeEventListener('keydown', kd); target.removeEventListener('keyup', ku);
         canvas.removeEventListener('pointerdown', pd); window.removeEventListener('pointermove', pm); window.removeEventListener('pointerup', pu);
         canvas.removeEventListener('wheel', wh); window.removeEventListener('blur', blur);
      }
   };
}
```

## `src/input/pointer.js`

```js
/**
 * Pointer state (mouse / touch / pen). Coordinates are canvas pixels with the origin at the
 * bottom-left (ShaderToy convention), computed from the canvas's *painted* box so they stay
 * correct with CSS scaling, devicePixelRatio and object-fit letterboxing.
 *
 * options.target: 'window' (default — hero text overlaying the canvas doesn't block input)
 *                 | 'canvas' | any Element
 * options.smooth: 0 (off) … 1   exponential smoothing applied per frame
 */
export function createPointer(canvas, getFit, options = {}) {
   const target = options.target === 'canvas' ? canvas
      : (options.target && typeof options.target === 'object') ? options.target
         : window;
   const smooth = +options.smooth || 0;

   const raw = { x: 0, y: 0, down: false, over: false, dragX: 0, dragY: 0, wheel: 0 };
   const state = { x: 0, y: 0, clickX: 0, clickY: 0, down: false, over: false, uvX: 0, uvY: 0, dragX: 0, dragY: 0, wheel: 0, clicked: false };
   let clickPending = false;
   let lastX = 0, lastY = 0;
   let moved = false;

   /** Painted rectangle of the bitmap inside the element box (accounts for object-fit). */
   function paintedRect() {
      const r = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
      const fit = getFit ? getFit() : 'fill';
      if (!r.width || !r.height || (fit !== 'contain' && fit !== 'cover')) return r;
      const ba = canvas.width / canvas.height, ra = r.width / r.height;
      let w, h;
      if ((ra > ba) === (fit === 'contain')) { h = r.height; w = h * ba; } else { w = r.width; h = w / ba; }
      return { left: r.left + (r.width - w) / 2, top: r.top + (r.height - h) / 2, width: w, height: h, bottom: 0, right: 0 };
   }

   /** client (DOM) coords -> canvas pixel coords, bottom-left origin */
   function toShader(clientX, clientY) {
      const r = paintedRect();
      if (!r.width || !r.height) return [0, 0];
      return [
         (clientX - r.left) * (canvas.width / r.width),
         (r.top + r.height - clientY) * (canvas.height / r.height)
      ];
   }
   const toUV = (cx, cy) => { const [x, y] = toShader(cx, cy); return [x / canvas.width, y / canvas.height]; };

   const onMove = e => {
      const [x, y] = toShader(e.clientX, e.clientY);
      if (raw.down) { raw.dragX += x - lastX; raw.dragY += y - lastY; }   // cumulative click-and-drag offset
      lastX = x; lastY = y;
      raw.x = x; raw.y = y;
      raw.over = x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x <= canvas.width && y <= canvas.height;
      if (!moved) { moved = true; state.x = x; state.y = y; }
   };
   const onDown = e => {
      if (e.button !== undefined && e.button !== 0) return;
      onMove(e);
      if (target === window && !raw.over) return;  // clicks outside the canvas don't count as a press
      raw.down = true;
      state.down = true;
      clickPending = true;
      lastX = raw.x; lastY = raw.y;
      state.clickX = raw.x; state.clickY = raw.y;
      if (!smooth) { state.x = raw.x; state.y = raw.y; }
   };
   const onUp = () => { raw.down = false; state.down = false; };
   const onLeave = () => { raw.over = false; };
   const onWheel = e => { raw.wheel += -e.deltaY * 0.002; };          // mouse-wheel accumulator (exp(wheel) makes a nice zoom)

   if (options.touch !== false && target === canvas) canvas.style.touchAction = 'none';
   target.addEventListener('pointermove', onMove, { passive: true });
   target.addEventListener('pointerdown', onDown, { passive: true });
   window.addEventListener('pointerup', onUp, { passive: true });
   window.addEventListener('pointercancel', onUp, { passive: true });
   target.addEventListener('pointerleave', onLeave, { passive: true });
   target.addEventListener('wheel', onWheel, { passive: true });

   return {
      state,
      toShader,
      toUV,
      /** Call once per frame. */
      update() {
         if (smooth > 0 && smooth < 1) {
            const k = 1 - Math.pow(1 - smooth, 1); // per-frame factor
            state.x += (raw.x - state.x) * k;
            state.y += (raw.y - state.y) * k;
         } else { state.x = raw.x; state.y = raw.y; }
         state.over = raw.over;
         state.clicked = clickPending; clickPending = false;   // true for exactly one frame after a press
         state.dragX = raw.dragX; state.dragY = raw.dragY; state.wheel = raw.wheel;
         state.uvX = canvas.width ? state.x / canvas.width : 0;
         state.uvY = canvas.height ? state.y / canvas.height : 0;
      },
      dispose() {
         target.removeEventListener('pointermove', onMove);
         target.removeEventListener('pointerdown', onDown);
         window.removeEventListener('pointerup', onUp);
         window.removeEventListener('pointercancel', onUp);
         target.removeEventListener('pointerleave', onLeave);
         target.removeEventListener('wheel', onWheel);
      }
   };
}
```

## `src/loaders/audio.js`

```js
/**
 * Audio -> spectrum texture (width = fftSize/2, 8-bit, read `.r`).
 * Row 0 = frequency spectrum, row 1 = waveform (ShaderToy layout). Height is 2.
 *   { audio: 'song.mp3', fftSize: 1024 }    file (loops, starts on first user gesture)
 *   { mic: true }                            microphone input
 *   { audio: audioElement }                  an <audio> element you control yourself
 */
export function loadAudio(gl, pool, ch, { mic = false } = {}) {
   const spectro = ch.layout === 'spectrogram';                 // FragCoord.xyz u_audio: 512x512, scrolling history
   const fftSize = ch.fftSize || (spectro ? 1024 : 512);
   const AC = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;

   return new Promise((res, rej) => {
      const ctx = new AC();
      const analyser = ctx.createAnalyser();
      analyser.fftSize = fftSize;
      analyser.smoothingTimeConstant = ch.smoothing ?? 0.8;

      const bins = analyser.frequencyBinCount;
      const wave = new Uint8Array(fftSize);
      // ShaderToy layout: bins x 2 (row 0 spectrum, row 1 waveform), LUMINANCE.
      // Spectrogram layout: bins x bins RGB, row 0 = newest (R weighted FFT, G waveform, B raw FFT), older rows above.
      const H = spectro ? bins : 2;
      const chans = spectro ? 3 : 1;
      const data = new Uint8Array(bins * H * chans);
      const rawFFT = spectro ? new Uint8Array(bins) : null;
      let rawAnalyser = null;
      if (spectro) { rawAnalyser = ctx.createAnalyser(); rawAnalyser.fftSize = fftSize; rawAnalyser.smoothingTimeConstant = 0; }

      const tex = gl.createTexture();
      const fmt = spectro ? gl.RGB : gl.LUMINANCE;
      const upload = () => {
         gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D, tex);
         gl.pixelStorei(gl.UNPACK_FLIP_Y_WEBGL, false);
         gl.pixelStorei(gl.UNPACK_ALIGNMENT, 1);
         gl.texImage2D(gl.TEXTURE_2D, 0, fmt, bins, H, 0, fmt, gl.UNSIGNED_BYTE, data);
         gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D, null);
      };
      gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D, tex);
      gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, gl.LINEAR);
      gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, gl.LINEAR);
      gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_WRAP_S, gl.CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
      gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_WRAP_T, gl.CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
      upload();

      let getTime = () => ctx.currentTime;
      let cleanup = () => { };

      const finish = () => {
         const resume = () => { if (ctx.state === 'suspended') ctx.resume(); };
         resume();
         window.addEventListener('pointerdown', resume, { once: true });
         window.addEventListener('keydown', resume, { once: true });
         res({
            texId: pool.add(tex, 'texture2D'),
            width: bins, height: H,
            analyser, context: ctx,
            getTime,
            update() {
               if (spectro) {
                  const row = bins * 3;
                  data.copyWithin(row, 0, data.length - row);        // scroll history up one row
                  analyser.getByteFrequencyData(rawFFT);               // smoothed ("weighted") spectrum
                  analyser.getByteTimeDomainData(wave);
                  for (let i = 0; i < bins; i++) { data[i * 3] = rawFFT[i]; data[i * 3 + 1] = wave[i * 2]; }
                  rawAnalyser.getByteFrequencyData(rawFFT);            // unsmoothed
                  for (let i = 0; i < bins; i++) data[i * 3 + 2] = rawFFT[i];
               } else {
                  analyser.getByteFrequencyData(data.subarray(0, bins));
                  analyser.getByteTimeDomainData(wave);
                  data.set(wave.subarray(0, bins), bins);
               }
               upload();
            },
            dispose() { cleanup(); ctx.close && ctx.close(); }
         });
      };

      if (mic) {
         navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true, video: false }).then(stream => {
            const src = ctx.createMediaStreamSource(stream);
            src.connect(analyser); // not connected to destination: no feedback loop
            if (rawAnalyser) src.connect(rawAnalyser);
            cleanup = () => stream.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
            finish();
         }).catch(rej);
         return;
      }

      const url = ch.audio || ch.audioFFT;
      const audio = url instanceof HTMLMediaElement ? url : new Audio();
      const owned = audio !== url;
      if (owned) Object.assign(audio, { src: url, crossOrigin: 'anonymous', loop: ch.loop !== false, autoplay: true });
      getTime = () => audio.currentTime;

      const connect = () => {
         const src = ctx.createMediaElementSource(audio);
         src.connect(analyser);
         if (rawAnalyser) src.connect(rawAnalyser);
         analyser.connect(ctx.destination);
         if (owned) {
            const go = () => { if (ctx.state === 'suspended') ctx.resume(); audio.play().catch(() => { }); };
            go();
            window.addEventListener('pointerdown', go, { once: true });
            window.addEventListener('keydown', go, { once: true });
            cleanup = () => { audio.pause(); audio.removeAttribute('src'); audio.load(); };
         }
         finish();
      };
      if (audio.readyState >= 3) connect();
      else {
         audio.addEventListener('canplay', connect, { once: true });
         audio.addEventListener('error', () => rej(new Error(`Failed to load audio "${url}"`)), { once: true });
         if (owned) audio.load();
      }
   });
}
```

## `src/loaders/cubemap.js`

```js
import { wrapMode, filterMode } from '../gl/params.js';

const FACE_NAMES = ['+X', '-X', '+Y', '-Y', '+Z', '-Z'];

/** urls: [posx, negx, posy, negy, posz, negz] */
export function loadCubeMap(gl, pool, urls, params = {}) {
   return new Promise((res, rej) => {
      if (!Array.isArray(urls) || urls.length !== 6) return rej(new Error('cubemap needs an array of 6 URLs'));

      const tex = gl.createTexture();
      gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_CUBE_MAP, tex);
      const wrap = wrapMode(gl, params.wrap);
      const filter = filterMode(gl, params.filter);
      gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_CUBE_MAP, gl.TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, filter);
      gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_CUBE_MAP, gl.TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, filter);
      gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_CUBE_MAP, gl.TEXTURE_WRAP_S, wrap);
      gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_CUBE_MAP, gl.TEXTURE_WRAP_T, wrap);

      const faces = [
         gl.TEXTURE_CUBE_MAP_POSITIVE_X, gl.TEXTURE_CUBE_MAP_NEGATIVE_X,
         gl.TEXTURE_CUBE_MAP_POSITIVE_Y, gl.TEXTURE_CUBE_MAP_NEGATIVE_Y,
         gl.TEXTURE_CUBE_MAP_POSITIVE_Z, gl.TEXTURE_CUBE_MAP_NEGATIVE_Z
      ];
      let loaded = 0, w = 0, h = 0, failed = false;

      urls.forEach((u, i) => {
         const img = new Image();
         img.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
         img.onload = () => {
            if (failed) return;
            if (!w) { w = img.width; h = img.height; }
            gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_CUBE_MAP, tex);
            gl.pixelStorei(gl.UNPACK_FLIP_Y_WEBGL, false);
            gl.texImage2D(faces[i], 0, gl.RGBA, gl.RGBA, gl.UNSIGNED_BYTE, img);
            if (++loaded === 6) {
               if (params.mipmap) gl.generateMipmap(gl.TEXTURE_CUBE_MAP);
               gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_CUBE_MAP, null);
               res({ texId: pool.add(tex, 'textureCube'), width: w, height: h, cubemap: true, update() { } });
            }
         };
         img.onerror = () => {
            if (failed) return;
            failed = true;
            gl.deleteTexture(tex);
            rej(new Error(`Failed to load cubemap face ${FACE_NAMES[i]}: "${u}"`));
         };
         img.src = u;
      });
   });
}
```

## `src/loaders/element.js`

```js
import { makeTexture2D } from './image.js';

const isCanvas = el => typeof HTMLCanvasElement !== 'undefined' && el instanceof HTMLCanvasElement;
const isVideo = el => typeof HTMLVideoElement !== 'undefined' && el instanceof HTMLVideoElement;

/**
 * Use an existing element as a texture: <canvas>, <video>, <img>, ImageBitmap.
 * Canvases and videos are re-uploaded every frame (set `dynamic: false` to upload once).
 * Great for feeding a p5 / three.js / 2D canvas into a shader:  channels: [myCanvas]
 */
export function loadElement(gl, pool, el, params = {}, env = {}) {
   const w = el.videoWidth || el.naturalWidth || el.width || 1;
   const h = el.videoHeight || el.naturalHeight || el.height || 1;
   const live = isCanvas(el) || isVideo(el);
   const dynamic = params.dynamic === undefined ? live : !!params.dynamic;
   const wrapper = makeTexture2D(gl, pool, el, w, h, params, env, { dynamic, flipDefault: live });
   if (isVideo(el)) wrapper.getTime = () => el.currentTime;
   // keep the reported size in sync with a canvas that is resized later
   if (dynamic) {
      const base = wrapper.update;
      wrapper.update = () => {
         wrapper.width = el.videoWidth || el.naturalWidth || el.width || 1;
         wrapper.height = el.videoHeight || el.naturalHeight || el.height || 1;
         base();
      };
   }
   return Promise.resolve(wrapper);
}

/** Wrap a WebGLTexture the user created elsewhere (same GL context). */
export function wrapTexture(gl, pool, ch) {
   const id = pool.add(ch.texture, ch.cube ? 'textureCube' : 'texture2D');
   pool.ref(id); // never deleted by us: the user owns it
   return Promise.resolve({ texId: id, width: ch.width || 1, height: ch.height || 1, cubemap: !!ch.cube, update() { } });
}
```

## `src/loaders/image.js`

```js
import { wrapMode, filterMode } from '../gl/params.js';
import { isPow2, warnOnce } from '../utils/util.js';

/**
 * Upload an image/video/canvas/ImageBitmap to a new 2D texture.
 * Returns a channel wrapper: { texId, width, height, update(), dispose? }.
 */
export function makeTexture2D(gl, pool, source, w, h, params = {}, env = {}, { dynamic = false, flipDefault = false } = {}) {
   const tex = gl.createTexture();
   gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D, tex);

   const flip = params.flipY === undefined ? flipDefault : !!params.flipY;
   // WebGL1 only supports clamp + no mipmaps on non-power-of-two textures
   const npotLimited = !env.isGL2 && !(isPow2(w) && isPow2(h));
   if (npotLimited && params.wrap && params.wrap !== 'clamp') {
      warnOnce('npot-wrap', 'Non-power-of-two texture in WebGL1: forcing wrap to clamp.');
   }
   const wrap = npotLimited ? gl.CLAMP_TO_EDGE : wrapMode(gl, params.wrap);
   const mip = !!params.mipmap && !dynamic && !npotLimited;
   const filter = filterMode(gl, params.filter);
   const minFilter = mip
      ? (params.filter === 'nearest' ? gl.NEAREST_MIPMAP_NEAREST : gl.LINEAR_MIPMAP_LINEAR)
      : filter;

   gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, minFilter);
   gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, filter);
   gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_WRAP_S, wrap);
   gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_WRAP_T, wrap);

   const upload = () => {
      gl.pixelStorei(gl.UNPACK_FLIP_Y_WEBGL, flip);
      gl.pixelStorei(gl.UNPACK_PREMULTIPLY_ALPHA_WEBGL, !!params.premultiplyAlpha);
      gl.texImage2D(gl.TEXTURE_2D, 0, gl.RGBA, gl.RGBA, gl.UNSIGNED_BYTE, source);
      gl.pixelStorei(gl.UNPACK_FLIP_Y_WEBGL, false);
   };
   upload();
   if (mip) gl.generateMipmap(gl.TEXTURE_2D);
   gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D, null);

   return {
      texId: pool.add(tex, 'texture2D'),
      width: w,
      height: h,
      source,
      update: dynamic
         ? () => {
            if (source.readyState !== undefined && source.readyState < 2) return; // media not ready
            gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D, tex);
            upload();
            gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D, null);
         }
         : () => { }
   };
}

export function loadImage(gl, pool, url, params = {}, env = {}) {
   return new Promise((res, rej) => {
      const img = new Image();
      img.crossOrigin = params.crossOrigin === undefined ? 'anonymous' : params.crossOrigin;
      img.onload = () => res(makeTexture2D(gl, pool, img, img.naturalWidth, img.naturalHeight, params, env));
      img.onerror = () => rej(new Error(`Failed to load image "${url}"`));
      img.src = url;
   });
}
```

## `src/loaders/index.js`

```js
import { loadImage } from './image.js';
import { loadCubeMap } from './cubemap.js';
import { loadVideo, loadCamera } from './video.js';
import { loadAudio } from './audio.js';
import { loadElement, wrapTexture } from './element.js';
import { loadKeyboard } from './keyboard.js';

/**
 * Load one external (non-pass) channel. Resolves to a wrapper
 * { texId, width, height, update(), cubemap?, getTime?, dispose? } or rejects.
 */
export function loadChannel(gl, pool, ch, env) {
   if (ch.texture) return wrapTexture(gl, pool, ch);
   if (ch.element) return loadElement(gl, pool, ch.element, ch, env);
   if (ch.url) return loadImage(gl, pool, ch.url, ch, env);
   if (ch.video) return loadVideo(gl, pool, ch.video, ch, env);
   if (ch.camera) return loadCamera(gl, pool, ch, env);
   if (ch.mic) return loadAudio(gl, pool, ch, { mic: true });
   if (ch.keyboard) return loadKeyboard(gl, pool, ch);
   if (ch.audio || ch.audioFFT) return loadAudio(gl, pool, ch);
   if (ch.cubemap) return loadCubeMap(gl, pool, ch.cubemap, ch);
   return Promise.reject(new Error('Unrecognised channel: ' + JSON.stringify(ch)));
}

/** Sampler type a channel will need in GLSL. */
export function channelSamplerType(ch) {
   if (!ch) return null;
   return ch.cubemap || (ch.texture && ch.cube) ? 'cube' : '2D';
}
```

## `src/loaders/keyboard.js`

```js
/**
 * Keyboard state texture, ShaderToy layout (also what FragCoord.xyz's u_keyboard describes):
 *   256 x 3, 8-bit, one column per key code:
 *     row 0  key currently held        (texelFetch(k, ivec2(code, 0), 0).x)
 *     row 1  key pressed this frame
 *     row 2  toggle (flips on each press)
 * Key codes follow ShaderToy: JS keyCode (letters are their uppercase ASCII, 32 = space, 37..40 = arrows…).
 *   { keyboard: true }   or   { keyboard: { target: element } }
 */
export function loadKeyboard(gl, pool, ch = {}) {
   const W = 256, ROWS = 3;
   const data = new Uint8Array(W * ROWS);
   const held = new Uint8Array(W);
   const pressed = new Uint8Array(W);
   const toggle = new Uint8Array(W);
   const target = (ch.keyboard && typeof ch.keyboard === 'object' && ch.keyboard.target) || window;

   const code = e => {
      if (typeof e.keyCode === 'number' && e.keyCode) return e.keyCode & 255;
      const k = e.key || '';
      return k.length === 1 ? k.toUpperCase().charCodeAt(0) & 255 : 0;
   };
   const onDown = e => {
      const c = code(e);
      if (!held[c]) { pressed[c] = 255; toggle[c] = toggle[c] ? 0 : 255; }
      held[c] = 255;
   };
   const onUp = e => { held[code(e)] = 0; };
   const onBlur = () => held.fill(0);
   target.addEventListener('keydown', onDown);
   target.addEventListener('keyup', onUp);
   window.addEventListener('blur', onBlur);

   const tex = gl.createTexture();
   gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D, tex);
   gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, gl.NEAREST);
   gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, gl.NEAREST);
   gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_WRAP_S, gl.CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
   gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_WRAP_T, gl.CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
   const upload = () => {
      gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D, tex);
      gl.pixelStorei(gl.UNPACK_FLIP_Y_WEBGL, false);
      gl.pixelStorei(gl.UNPACK_ALIGNMENT, 1);
      gl.texImage2D(gl.TEXTURE_2D, 0, gl.LUMINANCE, W, ROWS, 0, gl.LUMINANCE, gl.UNSIGNED_BYTE, data);
      gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D, null);
   };
   upload();

   return Promise.resolve({
      texId: pool.add(tex, 'texture2D'),
      width: W, height: ROWS,
      keyboard: true,
      update() {
         data.set(held, 0);
         data.set(pressed, W);
         data.set(toggle, W * 2);
         upload();
         pressed.fill(0);              // "pressed" lasts exactly one frame
      },
      dispose() {
         target.removeEventListener('keydown', onDown);
         target.removeEventListener('keyup', onUp);
         window.removeEventListener('blur', onBlur);
      }
   });
}
```

## `src/loaders/video.js`

```js
import { makeTexture2D } from './image.js';

/** Retry media playback on the first user gesture when autoplay is blocked. */
export function playOnGesture(media) {
   const p = media.play && media.play();
   if (p && p.catch) {
      p.catch(() => {
         const go = () => { media.play().catch(() => { }); };
         window.addEventListener('pointerdown', go, { once: true });
         window.addEventListener('keydown', go, { once: true });
      });
   }
}

/** Video file -> live texture. `{ video: 'clip.mp4', muted, loop }` */
export function loadVideo(gl, pool, url, params = {}, env = {}) {
   return new Promise((res, rej) => {
      const video = document.createElement('video');
      Object.assign(video, {
         src: url, crossOrigin: 'anonymous',
         loop: params.loop !== false, muted: params.muted !== false, playsInline: true, autoplay: true
      });
      video.addEventListener('error', () => rej(new Error(`Failed to load video "${url}"`)), { once: true });
      video.addEventListener('canplay', () => {
         playOnGesture(video);
         const wrapper = makeTexture2D(gl, pool, video, video.videoWidth, video.videoHeight,
            { ...params, flipY: params.flipY === undefined ? true : params.flipY }, env, { dynamic: true });
         wrapper.getTime = () => video.currentTime;
         wrapper.media = video;
         wrapper.dispose = () => { video.pause(); video.removeAttribute('src'); video.load(); };
         res(wrapper);
      }, { once: true });
      video.load();
   });
}

/** Webcam -> live texture. `{ camera: true }` or `{ camera: { facingMode: 'user' } }` */
export function loadCamera(gl, pool, params = {}, env = {}) {
   const constraints = typeof params.camera === 'object' ? params.camera : { facingMode: 'user' };
   return navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ video: constraints, audio: false }).then(stream => new Promise(res => {
      const video = document.createElement('video');
      Object.assign(video, { srcObject: stream, muted: true, playsInline: true, autoplay: true });
      video.addEventListener('loadedmetadata', () => {
         video.play().catch(() => { });
         const wrapper = makeTexture2D(gl, pool, video, video.videoWidth, video.videoHeight,
            { ...params, flipY: params.flipY === undefined ? true : params.flipY }, env, { dynamic: true });
         wrapper.media = video;
         wrapper.dispose = () => { video.pause(); stream.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop()); };
         res(wrapper);
      }, { once: true });
   }));
}
```

## `src/react/index.js`

```js
// React bindings (optional entry: `shadermate/react`).
//
//   import { ShaderCanvas } from 'shadermate/react';
//   <ShaderCanvas shader={frag} uniforms={{ u_hover: hover ? 1 : 0 }} style={{ width: '100%', height: '60vh' }} />
//
//   const [ref, sm] = useShader({ shader: frag }, []);   // bring your own <canvas ref={ref}>

import { createElement, forwardRef, useEffect, useImperativeHandle, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { createShader } from '../index.js';

/**
 * Create an instance on a canvas you render yourself. Re-creates when `deps` change.
 * Returns [canvasRef, instance | null].
 */
export function useShader(options, deps = []) {
   const ref = useRef(null);
   const [sm, setSm] = useState(null);
   const latest = useRef(options);
   latest.current = options;

   useEffect(() => {
      if (!ref.current) return undefined;
      const inst = createShader({ ...latest.current, canvas: ref.current });
      setSm(inst);
      return () => { inst.destroy(); setSm(null); };
      // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
   }, deps);

   // live uniform updates without a rebuild
   useEffect(() => { if (sm && options.uniforms) sm.set(options.uniforms); });
   return [ref, sm];
}

/**
 * Drop-in canvas. Props: shader | passes, uniforms (live), options (anything createShader takes),
 * onReady(sm), onFrame(info), onError(err), plus any <canvas> props (className, style…).
 * The forwarded ref receives the ShaderMate instance.
 */
export const ShaderCanvas = forwardRef(function ShaderCanvas(
   { shader, passes, uniforms, options, onReady, onFrame, onError, deps = [], ...rest }, outerRef) {
   const key = JSON.stringify([shader, passes && passes.length, ...deps]);
   const [ref, sm] = useShader({ ...options, passes: passes ?? shader, uniforms, onReady, onFrame, onError }, [key]);
   useImperativeHandle(outerRef, () => sm, [sm]);
   return createElement('canvas', { ref, ...rest });
});
```

## `src/ui/errorOverlay.js`

```js
import { resolveTheme } from './theme.js';

/**
 * Compile-error overlay drawn over the canvas (dev aid). Enabled by default on localhost
 * (errorOverlay: 'auto'); set errorOverlay: true/false to force.
 */
export function createErrorOverlay(canvas, theme) {
   const T = resolveTheme(theme);
   let el = null;

   function place() {
      if (!el) return;
      const r = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
      Object.assign(el.style, { left: r.left + 'px', top: r.top + 'px', width: r.width + 'px', height: r.height + 'px' });
   }
   const esc = s => String(s).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;');

   return {
      show(err) {
         if (!el) {
            el = document.createElement('div');
            el.style.cssText =
               'position:fixed;z-index:2147483646;box-sizing:border-box;overflow:auto;padding:16px;' +
               `background:${T.bgAlt}f2;-webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(6px);backdrop-filter:blur(6px);color:${T.text};font:13px/1.55 ${T.font}`;
            document.body.appendChild(el);
            window.addEventListener('resize', place);
         }
         const errors = err.errors || [];
         const title = esc(err.message.split('\n')[0]);
         let body = '';
         if (errors.length) {
            body = errors.map(e =>
               `<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:auto 1fr;gap:4px 12px;padding:10px 12px;border:1px solid ${T.border};border-radius:${T.radius};background:${T.bg};margin-bottom:8px">` +
               `<span style="color:${T.red};font-family:${T.mono};font-size:12px">line ${e.line}</span><span style="color:${T.textStrong}">${esc(e.message)}</span>` +
               `<span></span><code style="color:${T.label};font-family:${T.mono};font-size:12px;white-space:pre-wrap">${esc(e.source.trim())}</code></div>`).join('');
         } else if (err.log) {
            body = `<pre style="margin:0;padding:10px 12px;border:1px solid ${T.border};border-radius:${T.radius};background:${T.bg};color:${T.label};font:12px/1.5 ${T.mono};white-space:pre-wrap">${esc(err.log)}</pre>`;
         }
         el.innerHTML =
            `<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;margin-bottom:12px">` +
            `<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;height:22px;padding:0 9px;border-radius:11px;background:${T.redSoft};color:${T.redText};font-size:11.5px;font-weight:500">Shader error</span>` +
            `<b style="color:${T.textStrong};font-weight:600">${title}</b></div>` + body;
         el.style.display = 'block';
         place();
      },
      hide() { if (el) el.style.display = 'none'; },
      dispose() { if (el) { el.remove(); window.removeEventListener('resize', place); el = null; } }
   };
}
```

## `src/ui/stats.js`

```js
import { resolveTheme } from './theme.js';

/**
 * Stats overlay (FPS, frame time, CPU time, frame count, resolution).
 * Nothing is installed unless you ask: stats: true (visible) | 'key' (hidden until the key is pressed).
 */
export function createStats({ visible = false, key = 'F1', position = 'top-left', theme } = {}) {
   const T = resolveTheme(theme);
   const el = document.createElement('div');
   const [v, h] = position.split('-');
   el.style.cssText =
      `position:fixed;${v === 'bottom' ? 'bottom' : 'top'}:12px;${h === 'right' ? 'right' : 'left'}:12px;` +
      `z-index:2147483647;min-width:168px;padding:8px 10px;border:1px solid ${T.border};border-radius:${T.radius};` +
      `background:${T.bg}e6;-webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(10px);backdrop-filter:blur(10px);box-shadow:${T.shadow};` +
      `color:${T.text};font:12px/1.5 ${T.mono};pointer-events:none;display:none`;
   document.body.appendChild(el);

   const row = (label, value) =>
      `<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:14px"><span style="color:${T.label}">${label}</span><span style="color:${T.textStrong}">${value}</span></div>`;

   let shown = false;
   let accDt = 0, accCpu = 0, n = 0, lastDom = 0;

   function show(on = true) {
      shown = !!on;
      el.style.display = shown ? 'block' : 'none';
      accDt = accCpu = n = 0;
   }
   const toggle = () => show(!shown);
   const onKey = e => { if (e.key === key) { e.preventDefault(); toggle(); } };
   if (key) window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
   show(visible);

   return {
      show, toggle,
      isVisible: () => shown,
      update(info) {
         if (!shown) return;
         accDt += info.rawDt; accCpu += info.cpuMs; n++;
         const now = performance.now();
         if (now - lastDom < 250) return;
         lastDom = now;
         const ms = (accDt / n) * 1000;
         el.innerHTML =
            `<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:baseline;margin-bottom:4px"><b style="color:${T.accent};font-size:16px;font-weight:600">${(1000 / ms).toFixed(0)}<span style="font-size:11px;color:${T.label};font-weight:400"> fps</span></b><span style="color:${T.label}">${ms.toFixed(1)} ms</span></div>` +
            row('cpu', (accCpu / n).toFixed(2) + ' ms') +
            row('frame', info.frame) +
            row('time', info.time.toFixed(2) + ' s') +
            row('size', `${info.width}×${info.height}`) +
            row('passes', info.passes);
         accDt = accCpu = n = 0;
      },
      dispose() {
         if (key) window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
         el.remove();
      }
   };
}
```

export const DEFAULT_THEME = {
   font: '"Geist", -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", system-ui, sans-serif',
   mono: '"Geist Mono", ui-monospace, "SF Mono", Menlo, Consolas, monospace',
   bg: '#1f1f1f',
   bgAlt: '#181818',
   raised: '#2a2d2e',
   hover: '#37373d',
   border: '#3c3c3c',
   text: '#cccccc',

   number: '#4daafc',
   string: '#d1977f',
   red: '#e46962',
   redSoft: '#4e3534',
   redText: '#f9dedc',
   radius: '8px',
   shadow: '0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,.03) inset, 0 16px 40px -16px rgba(0,0,0,.7), 0 4px 14px rgba(0,0,0,.3)',
};

export function resolveTheme(t) {
   return Object.assign({}, DEFAULT_THEME, t && typeof t === 'object' ? t : null);
}

## `src/uniforms/apply.js`

```js
import { describeGLType, resolveUniformType } from './types.js';
import { isArrayLike, ShaderMateError, warnOnce } from '../utils/util.js';

/**
 * A uniform entry is either a plain value (`0.5`, `[1,0,0]`, `true`)
 * or a spec object `{ type?, value, transpose?, ...ui hints }`.
 */
export function isUniformSpec(v) {
   return v != null && typeof v === 'object' && !isArrayLike(v) && 'value' in v;
}

const num = v => (typeof v === 'boolean' ? (v ? 1 : 0) : v);

function toList(value, n) {
   if (typeof value === 'number' || typeof value === 'boolean') return new Array(n).fill(num(value)); // broadcast
   if (ArrayBuffer.isView(value)) return value;
   if (Array.isArray(value) && value.some(x => typeof x === 'boolean')) return value.map(num);
   return value;
}

/** Upload `value` to `loc` according to a descriptor from types.js. */
export function uploadUniform(gl, loc, d, value, transpose) {
   const n = d.n;
   switch (d.kind) {
      case 'f':
         if (n === 1 && typeof value === 'number') return gl.uniform1f(loc, value);
         return gl['uniform' + n + 'fv'](loc, toList(value, n));
      case 'i':
         if (n === 1 && (typeof value === 'number' || typeof value === 'boolean')) return gl.uniform1i(loc, +value);
         return gl['uniform' + n + 'iv'](loc, toList(value, n));
      case 'u':
         if (n === 1 && typeof value === 'number') return gl.uniform1ui(loc, value);
         return gl['uniform' + n + 'uiv'](loc, toList(value, n));
      case 'm':
         return gl['uniformMatrix' + n + 'fv'](loc, !!transpose, value);
   }
}

/**
 * Apply a name -> entry map using the compiled program's uniform info.
 * Types are optional: when omitted they come from the shader itself.
 */
export function applyUniformMap(gl, info, map, { strict = false, warnMissing = false, label = '' } = {}) {
   if (!map) return;
   for (const name in map) {
      const raw = map[name];
      const spec = isUniformSpec(raw) ? raw : null;
      const value = spec ? spec.value : raw;
      if (value === undefined || value === null) continue;

      const inf = info[name];
      if (!inf || !inf.loc) {
         if (strict) throw new ShaderMateError('Unknown uniform: ' + name);
         if (warnMissing) warnOnce('missing-uniform:' + label + ':' + name,
            `Uniform "${name}" not found in ${label ? `pass "${label}"` : 'shader'} (misspelled, undeclared, or optimized out).`);
         continue;
      }

      let d;
      if (spec && spec.type != null) {
         d = resolveUniformType(spec.type);
         if (!d) {
            if (strict) throw new ShaderMateError('Unknown uniform type: ' + spec.type + ' for ' + name);
            warnOnce('bad-type:' + name, 'Unknown uniform type', spec.type, name);
            continue;
         }
      } else {
         d = describeGLType(inf.type);
         if (!d) continue; // sampler or unsupported type
      }
      uploadUniform(gl, inf.loc, d, value, spec && spec.transpose);
   }
}
```

## `src/uniforms/types.js`

```js
// Uniform type resolution.
//
// A uniform `type` may be written as the GLSL type ('float', 'vec3', 'mat4', 'int[]', ...)
// or as the classic WebGL shorthand ('1f', '3f', 'Matrix4fv', ...). Case-insensitive.
// If no type is given at all, it's inferred from the compiled shader (see describeGLType).
//
// Descriptor: { kind: 'f' | 'i' | 'u' | 'm', n }  (n = components, or matrix dimension)

const ALIASES = Object.create(null);
const add = (desc, ...names) => names.forEach(n => { ALIASES[n.toLowerCase()] = desc; });

// float family
add({ kind: 'f', n: 1 }, 'float', 'number', 'f', '1f', '1fv', 'float[]', 'uniform1f', 'uniform1fv');
add({ kind: 'f', n: 2 }, 'vec2', '2f', '2fv', 'vec2[]', 'uniform2f', 'uniform2fv');
add({ kind: 'f', n: 3 }, 'vec3', '3f', '3fv', 'vec3[]', 'uniform3f', 'uniform3fv');
add({ kind: 'f', n: 4 }, 'vec4', '4f', '4fv', 'vec4[]', 'uniform4f', 'uniform4fv');

// int / bool family (bool uniforms are uploaded as ints)
add({ kind: 'i', n: 1 }, 'int', 'bool', 'boolean', 'i', '1i', '1iv', 'int[]', 'bool[]', 'uniform1i', 'uniform1iv');
add({ kind: 'i', n: 2 }, 'ivec2', 'bvec2', '2i', '2iv', 'ivec2[]', 'bvec2[]', 'uniform2i', 'uniform2iv');
add({ kind: 'i', n: 3 }, 'ivec3', 'bvec3', '3i', '3iv', 'ivec3[]', 'bvec3[]', 'uniform3i', 'uniform3iv');
add({ kind: 'i', n: 4 }, 'ivec4', 'bvec4', '4i', '4iv', 'ivec4[]', 'bvec4[]', 'uniform4i', 'uniform4iv');

// unsigned int (WebGL2)
add({ kind: 'u', n: 1 }, 'uint', '1ui', '1uiv', 'uint[]');
add({ kind: 'u', n: 2 }, 'uvec2', '2ui', '2uiv', 'uvec2[]');
add({ kind: 'u', n: 3 }, 'uvec3', '3ui', '3uiv', 'uvec3[]');
add({ kind: 'u', n: 4 }, 'uvec4', '4ui', '4uiv', 'uvec4[]');

// matrices
add({ kind: 'm', n: 2 }, 'mat2', 'matrix2fv', 'mat2fv', 'uniformmatrix2fv');
add({ kind: 'm', n: 3 }, 'mat3', 'matrix3fv', 'mat3fv', 'uniformmatrix3fv');
add({ kind: 'm', n: 4 }, 'mat4', 'matrix4fv', 'mat4fv', 'uniformmatrix4fv');

/** 'vec3' | '3f' | 'Matrix4fv' | ... -> descriptor, or null if unknown. */
export function resolveUniformType(type) {
   if (typeof type !== 'string') return null;
   return ALIASES[type.trim().toLowerCase()] || null;
}

// GL enum -> descriptor (numeric constants are identical across contexts)
const GL_TYPES = {
   5126: { kind: 'f', n: 1 },  // FLOAT
   35664: { kind: 'f', n: 2 }, // FLOAT_VEC2
   35665: { kind: 'f', n: 3 },
   35666: { kind: 'f', n: 4 },
   5124: { kind: 'i', n: 1 },  // INT
   35667: { kind: 'i', n: 2 }, // INT_VEC2
   35668: { kind: 'i', n: 3 },
   35669: { kind: 'i', n: 4 },
   35670: { kind: 'i', n: 1 }, // BOOL
   35671: { kind: 'i', n: 2 }, // BOOL_VEC2
   35672: { kind: 'i', n: 3 },
   35673: { kind: 'i', n: 4 },
   5125: { kind: 'u', n: 1 },  // UNSIGNED_INT
   36294: { kind: 'u', n: 2 }, // UNSIGNED_INT_VEC2
   36295: { kind: 'u', n: 3 },
   36296: { kind: 'u', n: 4 },
   35674: { kind: 'm', n: 2 }, // FLOAT_MAT2
   35675: { kind: 'm', n: 3 },
   35676: { kind: 'm', n: 4 },
};

/** Active-uniform GL type -> descriptor. Samplers etc. return undefined (managed via channels). */
export function describeGLType(glType) {
   return GL_TYPES[glType];
}

export const GL_FLOAT = 5126;
export const GL_FLOAT_VEC2 = 35664;
export const GL_FLOAT_VEC4 = 35666;
```

## `src/utils/util.js`

```js
export class ShaderMateError extends Error {
   constructor(message, details) {
      super(message);
      this.name = 'ShaderMateError';
      if (details) Object.assign(this, details);
   }
}

const warned = new Set();
/** console.warn once per key. */
export function warnOnce(key, ...args) {
   if (warned.has(key)) return;
   warned.add(key);
   console.warn('[ShaderMate]', ...args);
}

export const isBrowser = typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof document !== 'undefined';
export const isArrayLike = v => Array.isArray(v) || (ArrayBuffer.isView(v) && !(v instanceof DataView));
export const toArray = v => (v == null ? [] : Array.isArray(v) ? v : [v]);
export const isPow2 = n => n > 0 && (n & (n - 1)) === 0;
export const clamp = (v, lo, hi) => Math.min(hi, Math.max(lo, v));

/** `glsl` tagged template: no-op, but lets editors syntax-highlight inline shaders. */
export const glsl = (strings, ...values) => String.raw({ raw: strings }, ...values);

/** Parse a CSS color ('#f80', 'rgb(…)', 'red') or [r,g,b,a] into [r,g,b,a] 0..1. */
export function parseColor(c) {
   if (c == null) return null;
   if (Array.isArray(c)) return [c[0] ?? 0, c[1] ?? 0, c[2] ?? 0, c[3] ?? 1];
   if (typeof c === 'string' && isBrowser) {
      const cv = document.createElement('canvas');
      cv.width = cv.height = 1;
      const x = cv.getContext('2d');
      x.fillStyle = c;
      x.fillRect(0, 0, 1, 1);
      const d = x.getImageData(0, 0, 1, 1).data;
      return [d[0] / 255, d[1] / 255, d[2] / 255, d[3] / 255];
   }
   return null;
}

/** True when running on localhost / file: — used for dev-only defaults. */
export function isDevHost() {
   if (!isBrowser) return false;
   const h = location.hostname;
   return location.protocol === 'file:' || h === 'localhost' || h === '127.0.0.1' || h === '' || h.endsWith('.local');
}
```

## `src/view/sizing.js`

```js
// Canvas sizing.
//
//   size: 'canvas'   (default when you pass a canvas)  bitmap follows the canvas's CSS box — style it
//                    however you like (width:100%; height:60vh …) and ShaderMate keeps up.
//   size: 'window'   (default when no canvas is given)  fixed full-window canvas.
//   size: [w, h]     fixed internal resolution; CSS box is yours, `fit` decides how it's shown.
//
//   pixelRatio: 'auto' (default, capped by maxPixelRatio = 2) | number
//   scale: 1         extra render-scale multiplier (0.5 = render at half res, cheap on big heroes)
//   aspect: 16/9 | '16:9' | [16, 9]   lock the bitmap aspect; shown with object-fit
//   fit: 'contain' | 'cover' | 'fill'  how a fixed/aspect-locked bitmap fills its CSS box

function parseAspect(a) {
   if (!a) return 0;
   if (typeof a === 'number') return a;
   if (Array.isArray(a)) return a[0] / a[1];
   if (typeof a === 'string') {
      const m = a.split(/[:/x]/).map(Number);
      return m.length === 2 && m[1] ? m[0] / m[1] : Number(a) || 0;
   }
   return 0;
}

export function parseSizing(options = {}, hasCanvas = true) {
   const s = options.size ?? options.sizing;
   const cfg = {
      mode: hasCanvas ? 'canvas' : 'window',
      width: 0, height: 0,
      aspect: parseAspect(options.aspect),
      fit: options.fit || 'contain',
      pixelRatio: options.pixelRatio ?? options.dpr ?? 'auto',
      maxPixelRatio: options.maxPixelRatio ?? 2,
      scale: options.scale ?? options.renderScale ?? 1,
   };
   if (Array.isArray(s)) { cfg.mode = 'fixed'; cfg.width = s[0]; cfg.height = s[1]; }
   else if (typeof s === 'string') cfg.mode = s === 'screen' || s === 'fullscreen' ? 'window' : s === 'element' ? 'canvas' : s;
   else if (s && typeof s === 'object') { cfg.mode = 'fixed'; cfg.width = s.width; cfg.height = s.height; if (s.fit) cfg.fit = s.fit; }
   if (cfg.mode === 'fixed') cfg.aspect = cfg.width / cfg.height;
   return cfg;
}

export function createSizer(canvas, options = {}, hasCanvas = true) {
   const cfg = parseSizing(options, hasCanvas);
   const ratio = () => (cfg.pixelRatio === 'auto'
      ? Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, cfg.maxPixelRatio)
      : (Number(cfg.pixelRatio) || 1));
   let styled = false;

   function cssBox() {
      if (cfg.mode === 'window') return { w: window.innerWidth, h: window.innerHeight };
      return { w: canvas.clientWidth || window.innerWidth, h: canvas.clientHeight || window.innerHeight };
   }

   function measure() {
      if (cfg.mode === 'fixed') return { w: cfg.width, h: cfg.height };
      const box = cssBox();
      const k = ratio() * cfg.scale;
      let w = box.w, h = box.h;
      if (cfg.aspect) {
         if (w / h > cfg.aspect) w = h * cfg.aspect; else h = w / cfg.aspect;
      }
      return { w: w * k, h: h * k };
   }

   function applyStyle() {
      if (styled) return;
      styled = true;
      if (cfg.mode === 'window') {
         canvas.style.cssText += ';display:block;position:fixed;left:0;top:0;width:100%;height:100%;';
      }
      if (cfg.mode === 'fixed' || cfg.aspect) canvas.style.objectFit = cfg.fit;
   }

   return {
      cfg,
      /** 'contain' | 'cover' | 'fill' as currently applied (for pointer mapping). */
      fit: () => ((cfg.mode === 'fixed' || cfg.aspect) ? cfg.fit : 'fill'),
      setFixed(w, h) { cfg.mode = 'fixed'; cfg.width = w; cfg.height = h; cfg.aspect = w / h; styled = false; },
      /** Recompute; returns true when the bitmap size changed. */
      update() {
         applyStyle();
         const m = measure();
         const w = Math.max(1, Math.round(m.w)), h = Math.max(1, Math.round(m.h));
         if (canvas.width === w && canvas.height === h) return false;
         canvas.width = w;
         canvas.height = h;
         return true;
      },
      /** Call `fn` on window resize / canvas box changes. Returns a disposer. */
      observe(fn) {
         let pending = false;
         const schedule = () => {
            if (pending) return;
            pending = true;
            requestAnimationFrame(() => { pending = false; fn(); });
         };
         window.addEventListener('resize', schedule);
         let ro = null;
         if (cfg.mode === 'canvas' && typeof ResizeObserver !== 'undefined') {
            ro = new ResizeObserver(schedule);
            ro.observe(canvas);
         }
         return () => { window.removeEventListener('resize', schedule); if (ro) ro.disconnect(); };
      }
   };
}
```

## `src/view/visibility.js`

```js
/**
 * Tracks whether rendering is worthwhile: tab visible AND canvas in (or near) the viewport.
 * Saves battery on long pages with a hero at the top.
 */
export function createVisibility(canvas, { enabled = true, margin = '100px', onChange } = {}) {
   let tabVisible = typeof document === 'undefined' ? true : !document.hidden;
   let inView = true;
   const notify = () => onChange && onChange(tabVisible && inView);

   const onVis = () => { tabVisible = !document.hidden; notify(); };
   let io = null;
   if (enabled) {
      document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVis);
      if (typeof IntersectionObserver !== 'undefined') {
         io = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
            for (const e of entries) inView = e.isIntersecting;
            notify();
         }, { rootMargin: margin });
         io.observe(canvas);
      }
   }
   return {
      get visible() { return tabVisible && inView; },
      dispose() {
         if (enabled) document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVis);
         if (io) io.disconnect();
      }
   };
}
```

## `test/build.test.js`

```js
// Smoke-tests the *built* files in dist/ (`npm test` builds first).
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { readFileSync, existsSync } from 'node:fs';
import { createRequire } from 'node:module';
import vm from 'node:vm';

const dist = f => new URL(`../dist/${f}`, import.meta.url);
const pkg = JSON.parse(readFileSync(new URL('../package.json', import.meta.url), 'utf8'));

test('all expected artifacts exist', () => {
   for (const f of ['shadermate.js', 'shadermate.min.js', 'shadermate.esm.js', 'shadermate.esm.min.js', 'shadermate.cjs',
      'gui.esm.js', 'shadermate-gui.js', 'shadermate-gui.min.js', 'react.esm.js']) {
      assert.ok(existsSync(dist(f)), f);
   }
});

for (const f of ['shadermate.js', 'shadermate.min.js']) {
   test(`${f} (IIFE) defines the globals without touching the DOM`, () => {
      const sandbox = { console };
      vm.createContext(sandbox);
      vm.runInContext(readFileSync(dist(f), 'utf8'), sandbox);
      // The global is the module namespace: same names as `import { … } from 'shadermate'`.
      assert.equal(typeof sandbox.ShaderMate.createShader, 'function');
      assert.equal(typeof sandbox.ShaderMate.glsl, 'function');
      assert.equal(sandbox.ShaderMate.VERSION, pkg.version);
      assert.equal(sandbox.ShaderMate.create, undefined);          // no aliases
      assert.equal(sandbox.ShaderMate.default, undefined);
      assert.equal(sandbox.createShader, undefined);           // no loose globals
      assert.throws(() => sandbox.ShaderMate.createShader({ shader: 'x' }), /browser/);  // no window -> clear error
   });
}

test('ESM build exports', async () => {
   const m = await import(dist('shadermate.esm.js'));
   assert.equal(typeof m.createShader, 'function');
   assert.equal(m.default, undefined);                            // named exports only
   assert.equal(m.create, undefined);
   assert.equal(m.startShaderMate, undefined);
   assert.equal(m.VERSION, pkg.version);
   assert.equal(m.glsl`a${1}b`, 'a1b');
   assert.deepEqual(m.resolveUniformType('vec3'), m.resolveUniformType('3f'));
   assert.ok(m.BUILTIN_UNIFORMS.iTime);
});

test('CJS build exports', () => {
   const require = createRequire(import.meta.url);
   const m = require('../dist/shadermate.cjs');
   assert.equal(typeof m.createShader, 'function');
   assert.equal(m.VERSION, pkg.version);
});

test('addon builds keep peers and the core external', () => {
   const gui = readFileSync(dist('gui.esm.js'), 'utf8');
   const react = readFileSync(dist('react.esm.js'), 'utf8');
   assert.match(gui, /from "lil-gui"/);
   assert.doesNotMatch(gui, /class GUI/);                   // not bundled
   assert.match(react, /from "react"/);
   assert.match(react, /from "\.\/shadermate\.esm\.js"/);
   assert.doesNotMatch(react, /createContext\(canvas/);     // core not duplicated
   const guiIife = readFileSync(dist('shadermate-gui.js'), 'utf8');
   assert.match(guiIife, /lil-gui/);
   assert.match(guiIife, /ns\.createGui = createGui/);
});

test('package exports map matches files', () => {
   for (const key of ['.', './gui', './react']) {
      const e = pkg.exports[key];
      assert.ok(existsSync(new URL('../' + e.import, import.meta.url)), e.import);
      assert.ok(existsSync(new URL('../' + e.types, import.meta.url)), e.types);
   }
});
```

## `test/fragcoord.test.js`

```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { splitPassBlob, parseUniformComments, usesMouseVec4 } from '../src/glsl/fragcoord.js';
import { looksLikeFragCoord } from '../src/glsl/builtins.js';
import { assembleFragment } from '../src/glsl/preprocess.js';

test('splitPassBlob: Common / Pass N / Main headers', () => {
   const blob = `// Common
#define PI 3.14
// Pass 1
void main(){ fragColor = vec4(1.0); }
// Pass 2 — Blur
void main(){ fragColor = texture(u_pass1, gl_FragCoord.xy/u_resolution); }
// Main
void main(){ fragColor = texture(u_pass2, gl_FragCoord.xy/u_resolution); }`;
   const r = splitPassBlob(blob);
   assert.equal(r.common, '#define PI 3.14');
   assert.deepEqual(r.passes.map(p => p.name), ['pass1', 'pass2', 'main']);
   assert.equal(r.passes[2].main, true);
   assert.match(r.passes[1].src, /u_pass1/);
});

test('splitPassBlob: export style with named pass and buffer type', () => {
   const r = splitPassBlob(`// ===== Pass: Flow =====\nvoid main(){}\n// Pass: map — Buffer type: 3D\nvoid main(){}\n// Main\nvoid main(){}`);
   assert.equal(r.passes[0].name, 'flow');
   assert.equal(r.passes[1].bufferType, '3d');
   assert.equal(splitPassBlob('void main(){ fragColor = vec4(1.0); }'), null);
});

test('parseUniformComments: ranges, defaults, initializers stripped', () => {
   const src = `uniform float u_speed; //1.0, [0.0, 5.0]
uniform float u_x = 0.5; //[0, 5]
uniform vec3 u_color; //0.2, 0.55, 1.0, [0, 1]
uniform sampler2D u_tex; //https://example.com/a.png [linear repeat]
uniform sampler2D u_clip; //https://example.com/loop.mp4
uniform sampler2D u_prev; // ref:pass1 [nearest clamp]
uniform sampler2D u_audio; // mic
uniform float u_plain;`;
   const r = parseUniformComments(src);
   assert.deepEqual(r.uniforms.u_speed, { value: 1, min: 0, max: 5 });
   assert.deepEqual(r.uniforms.u_x, { value: 0.5, min: 0, max: 5 });
   assert.deepEqual(r.uniforms.u_color, { value: [0.2, 0.55, 1], min: 0, max: 1 });
   assert.equal(r.uniforms.u_plain, undefined);
   assert.deepEqual(r.samplers.u_tex, { url: 'https://example.com/a.png', filter: 'linear', wrap: 'repeat' });
   assert.deepEqual(r.samplers.u_clip, { video: 'https://example.com/loop.mp4' });
   assert.deepEqual(r.samplers.u_prev, { pass: 'pass1', filter: 'nearest', wrap: 'clamp' });
   assert.deepEqual(r.samplers.u_audio, { mic: true, layout: 'spectrogram' });
   assert.match(r.source, /uniform float u_x;/);
   assert.doesNotMatch(r.source, /= 0\.5/);
});

test('FragCoord dialect: builtins declared, u_mouse vec4, u_scroll float, u_passN samplers', () => {
   const src = `void main(){
  vec2 uv = gl_FragCoord.xy / u_resolution;
  vec3 c = texture(u_pass1, uv).rgb + u_mouse.z + u_scroll + float(u_frame) + u_camera_pos.x;
  fragColor = vec4(c, 1.0);
}`;
   assert.equal(looksLikeFragCoord(src), true);
   assert.equal(usesMouseVec4(src), true);
   const frag = assembleFragment(src, { isGL2: true, builtinOpts: { fragcoord: true, mouseVec4: true, scrollFloat: true, samplerNames: ['u_pass1', 'u_main'], passCount: 1 } });
   assert.match(frag, /uniform vec4 u_mouse;/);
   assert.match(frag, /uniform float u_scroll;/);
   assert.match(frag, /uniform int u_frame;/);
   assert.match(frag, /uniform sampler2D u_pass1;/);
   assert.doesNotMatch(frag, /uniform sampler2D u_main;/);
   assert.match(frag, /uniform vec3 u_camera_pos;/);
   assert.equal((frag.match(/out vec4 fragColor;/g) || []).length, 1);
});
```

## `test/passes.test.js`

```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { normalizePasses, normalizeChannel, isInlineSource } from '../src/core/passes.js';
import { resolvePassSize } from '../src/gl/targets.js';
import { parseSizing } from '../src/view/sizing.js';

test('string source -> single screen pass', () => {
   const [p] = normalizePasses('frag.glsl');
   assert.equal(p.name, 'main');
   assert.equal(p.screen, true);
   assert.equal(p.channels.length, 4);
});

test('last pass becomes the screen pass; caller objects are not mutated', () => {
   const input = [{ name: 'A', shader: 'a.glsl' }, { name: 'B', shader: 'b.glsl' }];
   const out = normalizePasses(input);
   assert.equal(out[1].screen, true);
   assert.equal(out[0].screen, undefined);
   assert.equal(out[0].src, 'a.glsl');
   assert.equal(input[1].screen, undefined);
});

test('uniforms object is shared by reference (live mutation keeps working)', () => {
   const uniforms = { u_x: { type: 'float', value: 1 } };
   const [p] = normalizePasses([{ src: 'a.glsl', uniforms }]);
   assert.equal(p.uniforms, uniforms);
});

test('errors: no source, duplicate names, bad input', () => {
   assert.throws(() => normalizePasses([{ name: 'x' }]), /no shader source/);
   assert.throws(() => normalizePasses([{ name: 'a', src: 'x' }, { name: 'a', src: 'y' }]), /Duplicate/);
   assert.throws(() => normalizePasses(42), /expected/);
});

test('channels: id (docs) and pass (code) both work; strings; nulls; aliases', () => {
   const names = ['BufferA', 'Image'];
   assert.equal(normalizeChannel({ id: 'BufferA' }, names).pass, 'BufferA');
   assert.equal(normalizeChannel({ pass: 'BufferA' }, names).pass, 'BufferA');
   assert.deepEqual(normalizeChannel('BufferA', names), { pass: 'BufferA' });
   assert.deepEqual(normalizeChannel('tex.png', names), { url: 'tex.png' });
   assert.equal(normalizeChannel({ image: 'x.png' }, names).url, 'x.png');
   assert.equal(normalizeChannel(null, names), null);
   const out = normalizePasses([{ name: 'BufferA', src: 'a' }, { name: 'Image', src: 'b', channels: [{ id: 'BufferA' }, null, 'tex.png'] }]);
   assert.equal(out[1].channels[0].pass, 'BufferA');
   assert.equal(out[1].channels[2].url, 'tex.png');
   assert.equal(out[1].channels[3], null);
});

test('inline GLSL detection', () => {
   assert.equal(isInlineSource('void mainImage(out vec4 c, in vec2 p){}'), true);
   assert.equal(isInlineSource('#version 300 es\nvoid main(){}'), true);
   assert.equal(isInlineSource('shaders/frag.glsl'), false);
   assert.equal(isInlineSource('https://example.com/a.glsl?v=2'), false);
});

test('pass sizes', () => {
   assert.deepEqual(resolvePassSize('screen', 800, 600), [800, 600]);
   assert.deepEqual(resolvePassSize('half', 800, 600), [400, 300]);
   assert.deepEqual(resolvePassSize(0.5, 801, 601), [401, 301]);
   assert.deepEqual(resolvePassSize([256, 128], 800, 600), [256, 128]);
   assert.deepEqual(resolvePassSize({ width: 64, height: 32 }, 800, 600), [64, 32]);
   assert.deepEqual(resolvePassSize('half', 1, 1), [1, 1]);
});

test('sizing option parsing', () => {
   assert.equal(parseSizing({}, true).mode, 'canvas');
   assert.equal(parseSizing({}, false).mode, 'window');
   assert.equal(parseSizing({}).pixelRatio, 'auto');
   assert.equal(parseSizing({ size: [640, 360] }).mode, 'fixed');
   assert.equal(parseSizing({ size: [640, 360] }).aspect.toFixed(3), '1.778');
   assert.equal(parseSizing({ aspect: '16:9' }).aspect.toFixed(3), '1.778');
   assert.equal(parseSizing({ aspect: [4, 3] }).aspect.toFixed(3), '1.333');
   assert.equal(parseSizing({ size: 'element' }).mode, 'canvas');
   assert.equal(parseSizing({ size: 'screen' }).mode, 'window');
   assert.equal(parseSizing({ scale: 0.5 }).scale, 0.5);
});
```

## `test/preprocess.test.js`

```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { assembleFragment, resolveIncludes, defineLines, DEFAULT_INJECT, NO_INJECT } from '../src/glsl/preprocess.js';
import { declareBuiltins } from '../src/glsl/builtins.js';

const quiet = () => { };
const ST = 'void mainImage(out vec4 c, in vec2 p){ c = vec4(iTime, iResolution.x, 0.0, 1.0); }';

test('GL2: version first, builtins declared, mainImage wrapped, #line mapping present', () => {
   const out = assembleFragment(ST, { isGL2: true });
   assert.ok(out.startsWith('#version 300 es\n'));
   assert.match(out, /out vec4 outColor;/);
   assert.match(out, /uniform float iTime;/);
   assert.match(out, /uniform vec3 iResolution;/);
   assert.doesNotMatch(out, /uniform vec4 iMouse/);        // unused -> not declared
   assert.match(out, /#line 1\nvoid mainImage/);
   assert.match(out, /outColor = c;/);
});

test('GL1: no version, #extension precedes precision, texture() macro, gl_FragColor wrapper', () => {
   const out = assembleFragment(ST, { isGL2: false, enableDerivatives: true });
   assert.ok(!out.includes('#version'));
   assert.ok(out.indexOf('#extension') < out.indexOf('precision'));
   assert.match(out, /#define texture\(s,u\)\s+texture2D\(s,u\)/);
   assert.match(out, /gl_FragColor = c;/);
});

test('user-declared built-ins are not re-declared; cube channels get samplerCube', () => {
   const src = 'uniform highp float iTime; uniform vec2 iResolution;\nvoid main(){ outColor = texture(iChannel0, vec3(1)) + texture(iChannel1, vec2(0)); }';
   const decl = declareBuiltins(src, ['cube', '2D']);
   assert.doesNotMatch(decl, /iTime/);
   assert.doesNotMatch(decl, /iResolution/);
   assert.match(decl, /uniform samplerCube iChannel0;/);
   assert.match(decl, /uniform sampler2D iChannel1;/);
   assert.match(declareBuiltins('float a = iChannelTime[2] + u_mouseUV.x;'), /uniform float iChannelTime\[4\];\nuniform vec2 u_mouseUV;/);
});

test('names inside comments do not trigger declarations', () => {
   assert.equal(declareBuiltins('// iTime iMouse\n/* iResolution */ void main(){}'), '');
});

test('does not wrap when user defines main(), even with mainImage( in a comment', () => {
   const out = assembleFragment('// mainImage( is cool\nvoid main(){ outColor = vec4(1.0); }', { isGL2: true });
   assert.equal((out.match(/void main\s*\(/g) || []).length, 1);
});

test('user #version is hoisted to line 1', () => {
   const out = assembleFragment('\n#version 300 es\nvoid main(){}', { isGL2: true });
   assert.ok(out.startsWith('#version 300 es\n'));
   assert.equal((out.match(/#version/g) || []).length, 1);
});

test('all injection off returns source untouched; DEFAULT_INJECT has every switch on', () => {
   const src = 'void main(){}';
   assert.equal(assembleFragment(src, { isGL2: true, inject: NO_INJECT }), src);
   assert.ok(Object.values(DEFAULT_INJECT).every(Boolean));
});

test('defines: strings, objects, true/value', () => {
   assert.equal(defineLines(['A', { B: 2, C: true }]), '#define A\n#define B 2\n#define C\n');
   assert.match(assembleFragment('void main(){}', { isGL2: true, defines: [{ N: 4 }] }), /#define N 4/);
});

test('includes: nested, include-once, circular detection, failures', async () => {
   const files = {
      'a.glsl': '#include "b.glsl"\nfloat a(){return 1.0;}',
      'b.glsl': 'float b(){return 2.0;}',
      'loop.glsl': '#include "loop.glsl"\nfloat l;',
   };
   const loader = async p => { if (!(p in files)) throw new Error('404'); return files[p]; };

   const out = await resolveIncludes('#include "a.glsl"\n#include "b.glsl"\n#include "nope.glsl"', { loader, warn: quiet });
   assert.match(out, /begin include "a.glsl"/);
   assert.equal((out.match(/float b\(\)/g) || []).length, 1);
   assert.match(out, /already included/);
   assert.match(out, /failed include "nope.glsl"/);

   const warnings = [];
   const cyc = await resolveIncludes('#include "loop.glsl"', { loader, warn: (...a) => warnings.push(a.join(' ')) });
   assert.match(cyc, /circular/);
   assert.ok(warnings.some(w => w.includes('Circular')));
   await assert.rejects(resolveIncludes('#include "nope.glsl"', { loader, warn: quiet, throwOnError: true }), /404/);
});

test('FragCoord.xyz style: fragColor from main() is declared; user-declared vec4 u_mouse is respected', () => {
   const src = 'uniform vec2 u_resolution;\nuniform vec4 u_mouse;\nvoid main() { fragColor = vec4(u_mouse.xy / u_resolution, u_time_delta, 1.0); }';
   const gl2 = assembleFragment(src, { isGL2: true });
   assert.match(gl2, /out vec4 fragColor;/);
   assert.doesNotMatch(gl2, /uniform vec2 u_mouse/);           // user's vec4 wins
   assert.match(gl2, /uniform float u_time_delta;/);
   assert.equal((gl2.match(/void main\s*\(/g) || []).length, 1); // no mainImage wrap
   const gl1 = assembleFragment(src, { isGL2: false });
   assert.match(gl1, /#define fragColor gl_FragColor/);
   // no fragColor injection when the shader uses mainImage (the wrapper owns the output)
   assert.doesNotMatch(assembleFragment('void mainImage(out vec4 fragColor, in vec2 fc){ fragColor = vec4(1.0); }', { isGL2: true }), /out vec4 fragColor;/);
});

test('exactly one fragment output is declared on GL2', () => {
   const count = out => (out.match(/\bout\s+vec4\s+\w+\s*;/g) || []).length;   // declarations only, not mainImage params
   assert.equal(count(assembleFragment('void main(){ fragColor = vec4(1.0); }', { isGL2: true })), 1);
   assert.equal(count(assembleFragment('void mainImage(out vec4 c, in vec2 p){ c = vec4(1.0); }', { isGL2: true })), 1);
   assert.equal(count(assembleFragment('out vec4 myOut;\nvoid main(){ myOut = vec4(1.0); }', { isGL2: true })), 1);
});
```

## `test/uniforms.test.js`

```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { resolveUniformType, describeGLType } from '../src/uniforms/types.js';
import { applyUniformMap, uploadUniform } from '../src/uniforms/apply.js';
import { createTweens, EASES } from '../src/core/tween.js';

test('GLSL names and classic shorthand resolve to the same descriptor', () => {
   const pairs = [
      ['float', '1f'], ['vec2', '2f'], ['vec3', '3f'], ['vec4', '4f'],
      ['int', '1i'], ['ivec2', '2i'], ['ivec3', '3i'], ['ivec4', '4i'],
      ['float[]', '1fv'], ['vec2[]', '2fv'], ['vec3[]', '3fv'], ['vec4[]', '4fv'],
      ['mat3', 'Matrix3fv'], ['mat4', 'Matrix4fv'], ['uint', '1ui'], ['bool', '1i'],
   ];
   for (const [a, b] of pairs) {
      assert.ok(resolveUniformType(a), a);
      assert.deepEqual(resolveUniformType(a), resolveUniformType(b), `${a} vs ${b}`);
   }
   assert.deepEqual(resolveUniformType(' VEC3 '), { kind: 'f', n: 3 });
   assert.equal(resolveUniformType('nope'), null);
   assert.equal(resolveUniformType(undefined), null);
});

test('GL enum inference', () => {
   assert.deepEqual(describeGLType(5126), { kind: 'f', n: 1 });
   assert.deepEqual(describeGLType(35665), { kind: 'f', n: 3 });
   assert.deepEqual(describeGLType(35676), { kind: 'm', n: 4 });
   assert.equal(describeGLType(35678), undefined); // sampler2D
});

// Records every gl.* call as [name, ...args]; read them back via `gl.calls`.
function mockGL() {
   const calls = [];
   return new Proxy({ calls }, {
      get: (t, name) => (name === 'calls' ? calls : (...args) => { calls.push([name, ...args]); })
   });
}

test('uploadUniform picks the right gl call', () => {
   const gl = mockGL();
   uploadUniform(gl, 'L', { kind: 'f', n: 1 }, 0.5);
   uploadUniform(gl, 'L', { kind: 'f', n: 3 }, [1, 2, 3]);
   uploadUniform(gl, 'L', { kind: 'f', n: 2 }, 7);            // broadcast
   uploadUniform(gl, 'L', { kind: 'i', n: 1 }, true);         // bool -> int
   uploadUniform(gl, 'L', { kind: 'm', n: 4 }, [0], true);
   assert.deepEqual(gl.calls.map(c => c[0]), ['uniform1f', 'uniform3fv', 'uniform2fv', 'uniform1i', 'uniformMatrix4fv']);
   assert.deepEqual(gl.calls[2][2], [7, 7]);
   assert.equal(gl.calls[3][2], 1);
   assert.equal(gl.calls[4][2], true);
});

test('applyUniformMap: inferred types, explicit aliases, specs, arrays, missing uniforms', () => {
   const gl = mockGL();
   const info = {
      u_a: { loc: 'A', type: 5126, size: 1 },
      u_c: { loc: 'C', type: 35665, size: 1 },
      u_i: { loc: 'I', type: 5124, size: 1 },
      u_t: { loc: 'T', type: 35666, size: 1 },
      u_arr: { loc: 'R', type: 5126, size: 3 },      // float[3]
   };
   applyUniformMap(gl, info, {
      u_a: 0.25,
      u_c: [1, 0, 0],
      u_i: { type: 'int', value: 3 },
      u_t: { type: '4f', value: [1, 2, 3, 4] },
      u_arr: [0.1, 0.2, 0.3],
      u_missing: 1,
   });
   assert.deepEqual(gl.calls.map(c => c[0]), ['uniform1f', 'uniform3fv', 'uniform1i', 'uniform4fv', 'uniform1fv']);
   assert.throws(() => applyUniformMap(gl, info, { u_missing: 1 }, { strict: true }), /Unknown uniform/);
   assert.throws(() => applyUniformMap(gl, info, { u_a: { type: 'wat', value: 1 } }, { strict: true }), /Unknown uniform type/);
});

test('tweens: numbers, arrays, interruption, easing names', async () => {
   const T = createTweens();
   let v = 0, c = [0, 0, 0];
   const p1 = T.tween('a', () => v, x => { v = x; }, 10, { duration: 100, ease: 'linear' });
   const p2 = T.tween('c', () => c, x => { c = x; }, [1, 2, 3], { duration: 100, ease: 'linear' });
   const t0 = performance.now();
   T.update(t0 + 50);
   assert.ok(Math.abs(v - 5) < 0.6, 'midway ' + v);
   assert.ok(Math.abs(c[2] - 1.5) < 0.2);
   T.update(t0 + 200);
   assert.equal(v, 10);
   assert.deepEqual(c, [1, 2, 3]);
   assert.equal(await p1, true);
   assert.equal(await p2, true);
   assert.equal(T.size, 0);

   const p3 = T.tween('a', () => v, x => { v = x; }, 0, { duration: 1000 });
   T.tween('a', () => v, x => { v = x; }, 5, { duration: 10 });   // interrupts p3
   assert.equal(await p3, false);
   for (const k in EASES) { assert.ok(Math.abs(EASES[k](1) - 1) < 1e-6, k); assert.ok(Math.abs(EASES[k](0)) < 1e-6, k); }
});
```

import type { ShaderInstance } from './index';
import type GUI from 'lil-gui';

export interface GuiOptions {
   title?: string;
   container?: HTMLElement;
   width?: number;
   open?: boolean;
   /** Playback folder (play/pause, time scale, screenshot, record). Default true. */
   controls?: boolean;
   /** true (all), false, or a list of uniform names to expose. */
   uniforms?: boolean | string[];
   /** false = leave lil-gui's default look; an object overrides parts of the theme. Defaults to the instance's theme. */
   theme?: false | Partial<import('./index').Theme>;
}

export function createGui(sm: ShaderInstance, opts?: GuiOptions): Promise<GUI>;
export function applyTheme(gui: GUI, theme: import('./index').Theme): GUI;
export { GUI };
